36. aṉittut toṭarvaṇṭikaḷum oru kiḻami kālattiṟku oṭavilli.

37. araciṉar untukaḷ patiṉintu nāṭkaḷukku ōṭavilli.

38. pañcāpilum tumukkic cūṭṭil nāṉku pēr iṟantaṉar.

39. mottam 50 untu vaṇṭikaḷ koḷuttappaṭṭaṉa.

40. putuvi aravintar pāḻi tākkappaṭṭu tī vikkappeṟṟatu.

41. intip paṭam kāṭṭātē eṉṟu nākarkōvil, ceṉṉi, kōvi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil koṭṭakikaḷiṉ muṉ pōrāṭit taṭuttu niṟuttiṉar.

42. ceṅkōṭṭiyarukil taṇṭavāḷam peyarkkappaṭṭatu.

43. kalkattāvilum pōrāṭṭam toṭaṅkiyatāl, paḷḷikaḷi 15 nāṭkaḷ mūṭumpaṭi kalkattā aracu āṇiyiṭṭatu.

44. āraṇi añcalakam cūṟiyāṭappaṭṭatu.

45. tiruttaṇiyil māṇavarkaḷ toṭarvaṇṭik kaṭavik katavukaḷi uṭittut tūḷ tūḷākkiṉar.

46. aṉantappūril ēṟattāḻa 3000 pēr toṭar vaṇṭi niliyattit tākkiṉar.

47. 12 vāṉūrtikaḷil paṭṭāḷam varavaḻikkappaṭṭatu.

48. kuṭiyēṟṟattil māṇavarkaḷukkum kāvalarkaḷukkum kikalappu nērntu, kāvalarkaḷ māṇavarkaḷ mēl 14muṟi tumukkic cūṭu naṭattiṉar.

49. kaṭiyanallūril tolivarik kampikaḷ aṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. añcalakap peyarp palakikaḷ uṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.

50. 1965-il naṭanta intiyetirppup pōrāṭṭattil kāvalarkaḷ kaṭṭatāl iṟantavarkaḷ mottam 68pēr. tīkkuḷippil iṟantavarkaḷ 5pēr. tīyiṭalil māṇṭavar oruvar. makkaḷāl tākkappaṭṭu māṇṭa kāvalarkaḷ pēr. cuṭappaṭṭu iṟanta 68 pēril kumārapāḷiyattil 15pēr; poḷḷācciyil 10 pēr; putuccēriyil 10 pēr.

1965ām āṇṭil naṭanta intippōril, māṇavar aṭarnteḻunta uṇarveḻucciyik kuṟittatu; ippāṭal. akkāl, tamiḻakattu, iṉaṅkākkavum moḻi pēṇavum niṉṟa kaṭcikaḷ palaviruntum, talivar palariruntum avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉālum avaruḷ oruvarālum tūṇṭap peṟāmal, tamiḻ kāttaṟkut tāmē eḻucciyuṟṟu vīṟukoḷa niṉṟa uyarpaḷḷi, kallūri māṇavartam valinta pōkki varalāṟu paṭuttuvatākum ippāṭal. iṉi, itu toṭarntu varum paṉṉiru pāṭalkaḷum ittakiyiṉavē.

itu, centamiḻt tumpi eṉ tiṇiyum puṉmoḻi porutal eṉ tuṟiyumām eṉka. tiṇi, tuṟi puṉinta putiyaṉa.

centamiḻkkeṉap poruta veḻuntatu ‘centamiḻttumpi'yum tamiḻ mīturntu aḻikka vanta intiyeṉ puṉmoḻi nōkkip poruti niṉṟatu ‘puṉmoḻi porutalu'm ām.





﻿52 ⁠tēṟuka neñcam





tēṟuka neñcam vīṟukoḷa niṉṟē!

īṟṟap polintatu niṉmaka ṉiciyē!

mūttōr marapiṉ yātta muttamiḻ!

kāttōr niraliṉ taliniṉ ṟaṉaṉē!



moympoṭu mutalā vammeṉ nōkkiṉ 5



kalvik kaḻakattu aiyaṟ ṟuṟanta

celvat taṉimakaṟ cērttiṉi amma!

veykuḻa leḵkam umiḻntuyir kaḻaṉṟa

meyyōṉ tamiḻccīr mīmici niṟutti,



aṟappūṇ viḷaṅkiya ākam paṉippa 10



maṟappūṇ payantaniṉ vayiṟu kuḷira

āyniṉak kuriyaṉ aṉivarkku mākiya

uḷaṅkiḷar mākkati uḷḷi

iḷaṅkiḷar uṇarviṉ ēntalaṉ ṟāyē!





poḻippu :

tēṟṟik koḷka (niṉ neñcatti perumi perumāṟu niṉṟu) aḻivu iṟuti illāmal viḷaṅki niṉṟatu niṉṉuṭiya makaṉatu pukaḻ. aṟiviṉ mutirnta toṉmiyōr tam vaḻivaḻi muṟimiyāl nilipeṟuvitta mūviyal tamiḻ moḻiyiṉik kaṭṭik kāttavar variciyil mutalāka niṉṟaṉaṉ, avaṉ! niṟinta valimiyoṭu, taṉṉic cērnta aṉivariṉum mutalvaṉāka varuka eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ, kalvi kaṟpikkappeṟum kalik kaḻakattu, taṉ tantiyit tuṟanta, uṭaṉ piṟappillā oru makaṉik kalvi kaṟka vēṇṭumeṉṟu cērttaṉi amma! veppamāṉa eḵkukkuḻalākiya vēṭṭeḵkattiṉiṉṟum umiḻap peṟṟa kuṇṭiṉāl, uyir nīṅkap peṟṟa uṭaliyuṭiyavaṉ, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ perumiyi mēlum uyarnilikkaṇ koṇṭu cērttu nilipeṟac ceytu, vāḻvaṟattiṉ ciṟappāl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya niṉ mārpu kaṇṇīrāl naṉiyum paṭiyum, maṟattiṟkē ciṟappu tōṉṟumāṟu niṉṟa avaṉip peṟṟeṭutta niṉ vayiṟu kuḷirum. paṭiyum, peṟṟa tāyākiya niṉakku uriyavaṉ, tāṉ ceyta tamiḻkākkum viṉiyāl tamiḻar aṉivarkkum uriyavaṉ ākip perumipeṟṟa, uḷḷattik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceyyum perumikkuriya varalāṟṟi niṉintu, iḷami kiḷarnteḻum uṇarviṉāl ciṟantōṉuṭiya tāyākiya nīyē!

virippu : ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattuk kalvik kālattut tamiḻ kākka muṉ niṉṟu uyir tuṟanta iḷaval aracēntiraṉ naṟṟāyit tēṟṟip pāṭiyatākum ip pāṭal. -

tēṟuka neñcam.niṉṟē! - perumitattāl uyarntu niṉṟu, niṉ neñcattit tēṟṟik koḷvāyāka. iḷaṅkiḷar uṇarviṉ ēntalaṉ tāyē, tēṟuka neñcam vīṟukoḷa niṉṟē” eṉṟu koṇṭu kūṭṭuka. niṉ makaṉ, piṟarpōl, iyaṟkiyāṉ varum potuvāṉa iṟappāl niṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟu viṭavilli. cemmāppuṟṟa oru koḷki nōkki ataṉic ceyaṟpaṭuttum poruṭṭāka uyir tuṟantaṉaṉ. eṉavē nīyum ‘ellāriyum pōl avaṉ iṟappukkāka varunta vēṇṭuvatilli.avaṉ peṟṟa perumiyi eṇṇi, niṉ aḻuṅkal neñcattit tēṟṟik koḷvāyāka eṉṟu teruṭṭiyatākum.

īṟṟap. iciyē aḻivāl muṭivuṟāta ciṟanta taṉmiyuṭiyatāy, ivvulaka iṟutivari viḷaṅki niṟpatākum niṉ makaṉuṭiya pukaḻ,

eṉṉi; moḻikku uṟuti cērkkum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭōṉatu moympukaḻ ammoḻi yuḷḷaḷavum niṉṟu nilipeṟuvatākumaṉṟō ! ākavē, tamiḻmoḻi ivvulakuḷḷaḷavum nilipeṟuvatu pōl, avaṉ pukaḻum niṉṟu nili peṟum eṉka.

mūttōr - aṟiviṉ mutirnta toṉmiyōr; cāṉṟōr.

marapiṉ yātta muttamiḻ - vaḻi vaḻi muṟimiyāl nili peṟuvitta mūviyal tamiḻ. yāttal nili peṟuvittal; kaṭṭu vittal eḻuttu, col, poruḷ, yāppu, aṇi ākiya aintuṟuppilakkaṇa vakiyāl kaṭṭuk kōppu ceyvikkap peṟṟa, iyal, ici, nāṭakameṉum mūviyalpāṉākiya tamiḻ moḻi!

eṉṉi mutaloli eḻuttukaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa veṉṟum, avi taḻuvi varum mūlac coṟ kūṟukaḷ ivi yivi eṉṟum, avaṟṟāṉ puṟappāṭuṟuvaṉavum, pulappāṭuṟuvaṉavum ākiya poruṇmoḻik kōḷkaḷ ivi yivi yeṉṟum, collum poruḷum niraluṟa niṟutti, uḷamum uṟaṉum tulakkuṟak kāṭṭi, aḻakum paḻakum uṇarvoṭu puṇara, kālamum iṭamum kaṭantu nili peṟuum, ciṟantōr neyyum ceyyuḷ amippukaḷ iviyivi yeṉṟum, avi ēyntu varum collum poruḷum miḷirat tōṉṟi, maṇivaki pōlum malki niṉṟa, aṇi vaki āvaṉa iviyivi yeṉṟum, tēṭṭamilārkkut teḷivuṟumāṟu kāṭṭi niṉṟa aintuṟuppilakkaṇam eṉka. eṉṟataṉpiṉ , niṉṟu nilaviya aṉṟiya naṟṟamiḻ, iyalpiṉ iyaṅkum uripeṟu kaṭṭuri, mayal tavir poruḷoṭu malkiya iyalum oliyoṭu veḻuttum, eḻuttoṭu collum, colloṭu poruḷum, poruḷoṭu paṇṇum, paṇṇoṭu tāḷamum, tāḷattōṭu iciyum ālattōṉṟi, kiḷappōriyum kēṭpōriyum uḷattālum uvaki mutaliya uṇarvukaḷālum piṇippikkum iciyum, ivviciyum iyalum acivuṟāniṉṟu, uṭalōṭiya uṇarviyum, uṇarvōṭiya cuvikaḷiyum oru naṉṉeṟikkaṇ kaṭṭi niṟutti, uḷattiyum uraṉiyum vaḷattakap paṭuttum nāṭakameṉṉum pūṭaka amippum, koṇṭu taḻuvit toṉṟu toṭṭu, kālattuk kālattu, iṭattu iṭattu, caḻakku nilimikku vaḻakkut tiriyiṉum puḻakku tamiḻ tōṟṟattup polivuṟa niṟkum mūvā iḷami muttamiḻ eṉka.

kāttōr niraliṉ ittaku perumi peṟumāṟu niṉṟa tamiḻi, niṉivu, moḻi, mey eṉṉum mūvurattāṉum kāttal viṉiyāṟṟuvōr variciyiṉ.

taliniṉṟaṉaṉē mutaliṭattu vittu eṇṇip peṟumāṟu iruntaṉaṉ.

moympoṭu - niṟinta valimiyoṭu.

mutalāvammeṉ nōkkiṉ-taṉṉic cārnta aṉittu māṇavariṉum mutalāmavaṉāka varuka eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ .

aiyuṟṟuṟanta celvat taṉimakaṉ- tantiyit tuṟanta uṭaṉ piṟappaṟṟa oru taṉimakaṉi.

kalvik kaḻakattuc cērttiṉi amma- kalvi payilum aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattil koṇṭu cērttiṉi.

veykuḻal eḵkam umiḻntu- vemmiyāṉa vēṭṭeḵkat tiṉiṉṟu umiḻap peṟṟa kuṇṭiṉāl,

uyirkaḻaṉṟa meyyōṉ- - uyir nīṅkappeṟ uṭaliyuṭiyavaṉ.

tamiḻccīr mīmici niṟutti tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ perumiyi mika uyarattil koṇṭu cērtti mēlum perumip paṭutti.

eṉṉi, tamiḻ moḻi vaḻaṅkum nilattil, vēṟṟu moḻiyākiya inti moḻi āḷumikku varuvatit taṭuttu niṟuttum nōkkattuṭaṉ pōrāṭi, appōrāṭṭattil taṉṉik kāvākat tantu, ap piṟamoḻi āḷumi muyaṟciyi muṟiyaṭittu, ataṉāl tamiḻukkut tēṭiya perumi. aḵtu iḻivi etirttup peṟṟatākaliṉ muṉṉiṉṟa perumiyiṉum mikunta perumi eṉka.

aṟappūṇ viḷaṅkiya ākam paṉip- vāḻvaṟattiṉ ciṟappāl viḷaṅki niṉṟa perumitam tāṅkiya mārpakam, viṭukiṉṟa kaṇṇirāl naṉintu kuḷira

maṟappūṇ - kuḷira maṟavuṇarvukkē ciṟapputtōṉṟumāṟu niṉṟa makaṉip peṟṟeṭutta vayiṟu, avaṉiṭṭiya pukaḻāl kuḷircci peṟumāṟu:

āyniṉak kuriyaṉ - tāyākiya uṉakkeṉa uriyavaṉ.

aṉivarkku mākiya- aṉittut tamiḻarkkum, avar moḻiyik kāttatāl, koṇṭāṭutaṟku uriyavaṉ ākiya

iḷaṅkiḷar uṇarviṉ ēntalaṉ- iḷami kiḷarnteḻum uṇarviṉāl ciṟantōṉ.

uḷaṅkiḷar mākkati uḷḷi - uḷattik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceyyum avaṉuṭiya perumi miku katiyi eṇṇi,

tāyē! - tāyākiya nīyē.

‘iḷami kiḷarnteḻukiṉṟa uṇarvuṭiya ēntalaṉ tāyē! tantiyaṟṟa niṉ oru taṉi makaṉi, avaṉiyotta māṇavarkaḷuḷ kalviyāṉ mutaliṭattē varumāṟu kalikkaḻakattuḷ cērttiṉi. avaṉ tamiḻ kākkum pōrāṭṭattuḷ taliniṉṟu, vēṭṭeḵkattāl cuṭappeṟṟut tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ perumiyi uyartti, niṉ maṉam varuntumpaṭiyum, niṉ vayiṟu kuḷirumpaṭiyum vīraṉāki uyir tuṟantāṉ. avvaḻi, niṉakku maṭṭumē uriyavaṉākiyirunta avaṉ tamiḻar aṉivarkkum uriyavaṉākip perumi peṟṟāṉ. uḷḷattik kiḷarnteḻac ceyyum ipperumiyuṭiya avaṉ vīra varalāṟṟi niṉintu, veṟṟiyuṇarvāl perumitam koḷḷumpaṭi, avaṉ maṟivāl tuyaruṟum niṉ neñcattik tēṟṟik koḷvāyāka!’ eṉṟu, tamiḻkkeṉa uyir cekuttōṉatu tāyit teruṭṭik kūṟiyatākum ippāṭal.

itu, centamiḻt tumpi eṉtiṉi māṇavaveṉṟi eṉ tuṟiyumākum.

tiṇi, puṉinta putiyatu. eṉṉi, māṇavaṉāki, niṟi paruvattēyē, vīraṉākip pukaḻ veṉṟāṉip pāṭiyatākaliṉ māṇavaveṉṟi āyiṟṟu eṉka.





﻿53⁠ maṟalkoṭu neñciṉ māmakaṉ





āṟṟilam eṉṟē aḻuṅkiṉar cāṉṟōr!

nōṟṟilam eṉṟē nontaṉar mutiyōr!

peṟṟilam eṉṟē āṭavar pulampiṉar!

tāyar ākilam eṉṟaṉar tāyar!

uṭappiṟan tilamē eṉmār iḷiyōr!⁠ 5

paṭaṟkilam nōkkeṉap pāviyar kalaṅkiṉar:

muntiṉam alameṉa nintaṉar uḻiyōr!

pintiṉam alameṉak kāvalar pitaṟṟiṉar!

mutirāc ciṟumoḻi muṉintu muttamiḻk

katireri eḵkam aṇimuṉ ṉiṉṟu⁠10

maṟalkoṭu neñciṉ māmakaṉ

viṟalpeṟat tāṅki vīḻnta ñāṉṟē!



poḻippu :

(ittaku vīṟu poruntiya maṟacceyali nām nam vāṇāḷil) āṟṟilamē eṉṟu uḷḷam varuntiṉar naṉṟu eṇṇi nallaṉavē ceyyum aṟiñar; (itu pōlum vīraṉi nām makaṉākap peṟutaṟku nām nōṉpu) nōṟṟa tilamē eṉṟu eṇṇi nōkaluṟṟaṉar, mutiyavarkaḷ; ivaṉaṉiya or āṇmakaṉi yām peṟṟirukkavilliyē eṉṟu) āṭavarkaḷ vāyaraṟṟip pulampiṉar; (ivaṉaṉiya oru maṟavaṉukku) nām tāyarāka villiyē eṉṟu kūṟiṉar,tāymār palar (ivaṉ pōlum oruvaṉoṭum) nām oru vayiṟṟil piṟakkavilliyē eṉṟu iḷiñarkaḷ urittaṉar : (ivaṉip pōlum oruvaṉiṉ) kaṇkaḷil nām paṭavum kātaluṟavum vāyppiliyē eṉṟu iḷanaṅkiyar maṉam varuntiṉar; (ivaṉ ceytatu pōlum oru maṟac ceyalic ceytaṟku nām) muntik koṇṭōm ilamē eṉṟu ivaṉ naṇparkaḷ uḷḷam tuyaruṟṟaṉar; (aiyakō, nam vēṭṭeḵkattāl veṭiyuṇṭu ivaṉ māyntiṭātavāṟu) nām nam ceyalil ciṟitu pintik koṇṭōm iliyē eṉṟu (ivaṉic cuṭṭa) kāvalar māṟumoḻi kūṟi vāy pitaṟṟiṉar; aṇmiyil tōṉṟi, ilakkṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl muṟṟi mutircciyuṟarat ciṟumi moḻiyākiya intiyi-ataṉ tiṇippip poṟuttuk koḷḷāmal, ciṉaṅkoṇṭu, muttamiḻ nalaṉukkāka, tīkkatirkaḷi umiḻkiṉṟa vēṭṭeḵkam(tumukkikaḷ) cūḻa vitta kāvalarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar añcāmal etirēṟi niṉṟu; vīraṅkoṇṭa neñciyuṭiya perumakaṉākiya aracēntiraṉ eṉṉum māṇavaṉ tāṉ koṇṭa koḷki veṟṟi peṟumpaṭi, taṉmēl vantu tākkiya eḵkuk kuṇṭukaḷit tāṅki, ataṉāl uyir pirintu vīḻnta anta poḻutilē!

virippu:

ip pāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

mutirāc ciṟumoḻi inti muṉintu, muttamiḻ kākka muṉṉiṉṟu, vēṭṭeḵkam tākki vīḻnta, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻaka aracēntiraṉip pāṭiyatu, itu.

intiyetirppil uyir nītta aracēntiraṉiyum avaṉ vīrac ceyaliyum, kēṭṭapoḻutu, cāṉṟōrum, mutiyōrum, āṭavarum, aṉṉiyarum, iḷiyōrum, pāviyarum, uḻiyōrum, kāvalarum aḻuṅkalākavum, āṟṟilarākavum, iraṅkalākavum eṉṉeṉṉa kūṟi varuntiṉar eṉpati ippāṭal terivikkiṉṟatu.

‘ittaku vīṟumikka oru vīram poruntiya ceyali nām nam vāḻnāṭkaṇ ceytatilliyē’ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr āṟṟāmiyāl varuttiṉar.

mutiyavarkaḷ 'ittakiya vīraṉ oruvaṉi nām makaṉākap peṟutaṟkut ‘tavam ceyya villiyē’ eṉṟu maṉam nontu kūṟiṉar.

‘ivaṉ pōlum ōr āṇmakaṉi nām peṟāmaṟ pōṉōmē eṉṟu āṭavarkaḷ pulampik koṇṭaṉar.

‘ittakiya vīraṉ oruvaṉukku nām tāyāka villiyē’ eṉṟu aṉṉimār maṉam varuntiṉar.

‘iṉṉavaṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ nām uṭaṉ piṟantilamē ‘ eṉṟu iḷiñarkaḷ varuntiṉar.

‘ivaṉik kātal ceyyum vāyppu namakku kiṭṭiliyē eṉṟu kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ kalaṅkiṉar.

ivaṉuṭaṉ.tōḻarkaḷāka nām iruntum, ivvīracceyalukku nām muntikkoḷḷavilliyē’ eṉṟu avaṉ tōḻaṉmār neñcam nintaṉar.

‘aiyakō, ittaku oru maṟavaṉi nām koṉṟuviṭṭōmē! nām caṟṟup pinti vantirāmaṟ pōṉōmē! akkāl ivaṉ aṭipaṭṭuc cākāmal irukka vāyppēṟpaṭṭirukkumē ! eṉṟu kāvalarkaḷum palavāṟu pittup piṭittavar pōlum vāyaraṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.

-ivvāṟu palarum, av vīramakaṉākiya aracēntiraṉ, inti moḻip pōril kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu uyir tuṟantapoḻutu, palavāṟāka eṇṇi, urittut tam varuttattit tīrttuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟavāṟu eṉka.

āṟṟilam - āṟṟi ilam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ puṇarcciyil iṭik kuṟintu niṉṟaṉa. āṟṟutal naṉṟāṟṟutal - nallatu ceytal.

aḻuṅkiṉar- varuntiṉar, neñcuḷ varuntutal.

cāṉṟōr - cāṉṟāki niṟpavar. piṟarkku eṭuttukkāṭṭāki niṟkum peruntakiyāḷar.

nōṟṟilam-nōṟṟu ilam-nōṉpu ceytilam mutiyōr nontaṉar eṉṟiruttaliṉ, mutumiyil kaṭanta kālattiṟku varuntutal eṉka.

peṟṟilam- peṟṟu ilam peṟṟiruttal illēm. peṟaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṉar avarākaviṉ āṭavar pulampiṉar āyiṉar.

tāyar ākilam tāyar: veṟumaṉē tāyar ākilam eṉiṉ, tāyppēṟē vāyātavar eṉṟākumākaliṉ, aḵtillāmi kuṟikkat tāyar eṉṟu tam pēṟu kuṟittaṉar eṉka.

uṭap piṟantilam - uṭaṉ piṟantilam eṉpaṉa valittup puṇarntaṉa

paṭaṟkilam nōkku - nōkkil paṭutaṟku ilam.

kalaṅkutal - maṉamum kaṇṇum orucērak kalaṅki varuntutal.

uḻiyōr - pāṅkuḷār. naṇpar.

pintiṉam-pitaṟṟiṉar' pintiṉōm allam eṉal nikaḻcci naṭanta piṉṉik kūṟutalākaliṉ pitaṟṟutal āṉatu.

mutirāc ciṟumoḻi - vaḷarcciyuṟāta ciṟiya payaṉtarum inti moḻi.

muṉintu- ataṉ pukutalik kaṭintu veṟuttu.

muttamiḻkku atir eri eḵkam aṇimuṉ niṉṟu - muttamiḻ kāttaliṉ poruṭṭu, atirkiṉṟa oliyuṭaṉ eriyumiḻkiṉṟa vēṭṭuk kuḻal tāṅkiya aṇiyiṉar muṉṉē tuṇintu niṉṟu.

maṟal koṭu neñciṉ māmakaṉ - vīram koṇṭa neñcuṭiya perumi mikkavaṉ.

viṟalpeṟat tāṅki - veṟṟi peṟumpaṭi neñcil ēṟṟu.

vīḻnta ñāṉṟu - uyir kaḻaṉṟu viḻunta poḻutu,

ippāṭal, 1965il tamiḻakattil naṭanta intimoḻit tiṇippi etirtteḻunta pōril, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattil aracēntiraṉ eṉṉum māṇavaṉ kāval cūṭṭit tāṅki maṭintapoḻutu, avaṉ vīrap piṟappip palarum pārāṭṭiyurittatik kēṭṭup pāṭiyatu.

itu, centamiḻt tumpi eṉ tiṇiyum, moḻi maṟam eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka. tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿54⁠ kōṇi araciyal





kavuḷnaṉi vēḻam kaṇipaṭ ṭāṅku

ceyirvumē lōṅkiya kalvic cevviyar

orutali māmaṇi ukuttōṉ peyarnta

maṉimukat tavaṉṟaṇi īṭṭaṅ kuvittu

muḻaṅku viṟaṟpaṟi muruka mūṭṭum⁠ 5

aḻaṟpōl vevveri aṭarak koḷuttiya

kāṭci kaṇṭōr tamiḻkaṇ ṭōrē

pūṭki aṟintōr tamiḻaṟin tōrē!

poṉṟiya tamiḻccīr putukki malarttum

māṇavar aṟaviṉi makiḻār⁠ 10

kōṇi araciyal koḷkiyi ṉōrē!





poḻippu :

kacikiṉṟa matanīrāl katuppu (kaṉṉam) naṉiyap peṟṟa āṇ yāṉi, eyta ampiṉālum kuttuppaṭṭa viṭattu, aṭaṅkāc ciṉam mēleḻum taṉmiyil (aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattuk) kalvi payila vanta māṇākkarkaḷ, taṅkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākiya talimittiṟam poruntiya ciṟanta oḷi maṇi pōṉṟa vīraṉ aracēntiraṉic cuṭṭu vīḻttac ceytōṉākiya kaḻakap pativāḷaṉ, (muṉṉarē añcit taṉ vāḻiṭamākiya maṉiyit tuṟantu puṟam pōkiya piṉṉi, am maṉiyiṉ muṉṟilil, (avaṉ uḷḷē viṭṭuc ceṉṟa maṉipporuḷkaḷākiya) ciṟappuṭiya īṭṭaṅkaḷi (katavi uḷḷē ceṉṟu, koṇṭu vantu pōṭṭuk) kuvittu, toṭarntu muḻaṅkukiṉṟa veṟṟi muracam, (īrattāl taḷarvuṟṟu muḻakkolikuṟiya, ataṉi mīṇṭum viṟippēṟṟutaṟ poruṭṭut tīk koḷuvikkāṭṭi) murukuvikka muṭṭukiṉṟataḻal pōl vemmi mikunta eriyiṉi aṭarntu eriyumpaṭi koḷutti mūṭṭiya ak kāṭciyik kaṇṭavarkaḷtām, (avarkaḷ ciṉantu pōrāṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka virunta tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiṉi, avvuṇarviṉ vaḻi) uṇmiyil kaṇṭavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ; avarkaḷiṉ uṟutippāṭṭi (iv vakiyāṉa ceyal vaḻi nēril kaṇṭu) aṟintavarkaḷ tām, (avarkaḷiṉ eḻuccikku aṭippaṭiyāka virunta tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiṉi uṇmiyilēyē aṟintavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ.

kuṟintu viṭṭa tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷi mīṇṭum putumi ceytu malarcciyuṟac ceyyum māṇavarkaḷiṉ, ivvalinta muyaṟci (aḻiviṉ pāṟ paṭṭataṉṟu); aṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭa ceyalē eṉṟu karuti makiḻātavarkaḷ (evarēṉum iruppiṉ avarkaḷ) koṭumiyāṉa vallatikāra araciyal koḷki uṭiyavarkaḷākavē iruttal vēṇṭum.

virippu :

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattut tamiḻttiṟaṅ kākka veḻunta māṇavaruḷ aracēntiraṉik kolluvitta kaḻakap pativāḷartam uṭimiyit tīkkoḷuviyaḻitta māṇavar tantiṟam viyantu pāṭiyatu itu.

aracēntiraṉ, kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu māyntāṉ eṉṟa ceyti kēṭṭatum, māṇavarkaḷ , itaṟkellām aṭippaṭi, palkalik kaḻaka vaḷākattiṟkuḷ kāvalarkaḷi varaviṭṭa pativāḷariṉ ceyalaṉṟō eṉṟu eṇṇi, vekuṇṭeḻuntu, avar vīṭu ceṉṟaṉar. cellavum, kaḻakap pativāḷar ataṉ muṉpē, tam vīṭṭip pūṭṭiviṭṭuk kuṭumpattuṭaṉ veḷiyēṟiyiruntatik kaṇṭaṉar. kāṇavum, mīṇṭum kotippu mēliṭṭavarāy avar vīṭṭi uṭittu, uḷḷē irunta avariṉ uṭimikaḷiyellām veḷiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭu vantu pōṭṭuk kuvittut tī vittuk koḷuttic cāmpalākkiṉar. inta nikaḻcciyi nēril pārttavarkaḷ, am māṇavarkaḷiṉ eḻuccikkut tamiḻ aṉṟō kāraṇam eṉa viyantaṉar. anta niliyi nēril kaṇṭu viyantu eḻutiyatākum ippāṭal.

kavuḷ - yāṉiyiṉ katuppu, kaṉṉam

naṉivēḻam - mata nīrāl naṉikkappaṭṭa āṇyāṉi,

kaṇi paṭṭāṅku - ēṟkaṉavē matam piṭittuḷḷa āṇ yāṉiyiṉ mēl, ampu tittatu pōl.

ceyirvu- kaṭuñciṉam.

kalvic cevviyar - kalvi payilum māṇākkarkaḷ. māṇpu eṉṉuñ colliṉ aṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiya māṇākkar eṉṉuñ collukku iṇiyāṉa putuccol, cevviyar.

oru tali mā maṇi - māṇavarkaḷuḷ tali ciṟanta vīraṉākiya aracēntiraṉ.

ukuttōṉ - uyiri ukukkac ceytōṉākiya pativāḷaṉ.

peyartta- veḷiyēṟip puṟampōkiya, evarkkum teriviyātu ceṉṟami peyartal eṉalāyiṟṟu.

maṉimukattu.kuvittu- pativāḷaṉiṉ maṉiyiṉ muṉṟilil avaṉatu taṉi īṭṭamākiya maṉiyaṭi poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu vantu kuvittu, taṉiyīṭṭam eṉṟatu avaṉ alvaḻiyil tokutta kuṟippāyiṟṟu.

kuvittu- oṉṟeṉak kūṭṭi

muḻaṅku viṟal paṟi- veṟṟip paṇ muḻaṅkit toynta paṟit tōl,

muruka mūṭṭum aḻal pōl - paṟit tōliṉ toyvu nīṅkic cūṭēṟi viṟippuṟutaṟku mūṭṭukiṉṟa eri neruppup pōl,

vevveri aṭara - veppamāṉa tī nākkukaḷ neruṅki eriya.

kāṭci kaṇṭōr - akkāṭciyiṉi nēril kaṇṭavarkaḷē ;

tamiḻ kaṇṭōrē - uṇmiyil tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ āṟṟalik kaṇṭavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ.

māṇavarkaḷ pativāḷaṉiṉ uṭimikaḷi avar vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭuvantu pōṭṭuk kuvittut tīmūṭṭi erittatu. avarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyi inti nuḻippiṉiṉṟu kākka veḻunta eḻucciyiṉāl ātaliṉ, avveḻuccikkuk kāraṇamākiya tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ uṇarvāṟṟālē māṇavarkaḷiṉ uḷḷūra niṉṟatu, eṉṟatu.

pūṭki - koḷki uṟuti.

pūṭki.... tamiḻaṟintōrē!- māṇavarkaḷiṉ koḷki uṟutiyi nēril aṟintavarkaḷtām. (ataṟkuk kāraṇamākiya tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷ ivvaḷavu uṟutiyit tara vallaṉavā eṉṟu eṇṇit) tamiḻi uṇmiyil aṟintavarkaḷ āvārkaḷ.

poṉṟiya - kuṟinta, maṟinta

tamiḻccīr- tamiḻ moḻiyiṉatu paḻimi, putumi, mutumi, iḷami, taṉimi, iṉimi, tiṇmi, nuṇmi, pukaṉmi, (pukalun taṉmi) , akaṉmi (virinta taṉmi) uṇmi,eṇmi(eḷimi), nayaṉmi (virumpun taṉmi), iyaṉmi, (iyaṟkit taṉmi), tāymi, tūymi, viyaṉmi, payaṉmi, (payaṉpaṭum taṉmi), eḻiṉmi, poḻiṉmi (poruḷ tarum taṉmi), iḻumi(iḷakal taṉmi), muḻumi (niṟinta taṉmi), cemmi, mummi (iyal, ici nāṭakam eṉum mūṉṟiyal taṉmi), cīrmi, ōrmi (ōriyal taṉmi-orumit taṉmi) ākiya irupattu āṟu ciṟappiyalkaḷi),

putukki-(ivvāṟu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ cīrmikaḷ palavāka iruppiṉum, avi nalintum melintum, tirintum ticintum, maṟintum kuṟintum, maṟantum iṟantum kāṇappaṭum taṉmikaḷic cari ceytu avaṟṟit taṟkāla aṟiviyal vaḷarccik kēṟpap) putumi ceytu,

malarttum - ataṉi etirkāla nilippāṭṭukku uriya vakiyil virivum malarcciyum uṟumpaṭi ceykiṉṟa.

māṇavar aṟaviṉi - (māṇavartam eḻucciyi vaṉmuṟi eṉṟu ikaḻāmal, putiyatōr ulaku ceyyum puraṭcip paṇiyākiya) kūrtalaṟa mēmpāṭṭu uḻippu eṉṟu karuti.

makiḻār - ivvāṟu or eḻucci vantatē eṉṟu eṇṇi makiḻa māṭṭātavarkaḷ.

kōṇi araciyal - nērallāmal vaḷinta koḷkikaḷiyum naṭimuṟikaḷiyum kaṭippiṭittu naṭattap peṟum ikkāla vallāṇmi araciyal taṉmiyiṉ,

koḷkiyiṉōrē - koḷkiyik koṇṭavarkaḷē!

ippāṭaliṉ varalāṟṟu nikaḻviṉi muṉṉiya mūṉṟupāṭal kaḷāṉum urikaḷāṉum kaṇṭu koḷka.

māṇavarkaḷiṉ eḻucci aṟamuṭiya ceyalaṉṟu eṉṟu maṉamum, mukamum cuḷukkuvār, kitērnta araciyal karavuṭiyavarkaḷē aṉṟi, uṇmiyiṉ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiyalkaḷi aṟintārallar eṉṟu teḷippāṉ vēṇṭi urittatu eṉka.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum, mutumoḻipēṇal eṉtuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





aṇṇā malicey palkalik kaḻakattu

uṇṇil pōkkiya iḷiyōr aṇmi

teruvalam vantu cūḷuri muḻakki

oḷḷiya koḷki cuvartoṟum varintu

miniṟa neṭuṅkoṭi paṉmaṉi tūkki⁠ 5

neṭuntoṭar vaṇṭit taṭampaṭut tiruntu

kallāp pulimoḻi kaṟkeṉat tūṇṭiya

ollāp pētiyar aracu kaṭintu

kāval māṭattu ēval paṇintu

valciṟi pōki meyyaṭi paṭṭu⁠ 10

neruppuri yāṟṟi uṟuppaṟi pōkiya

taṭantōḷ māṇavark kuḷḷuyir kīṇṭa

tāḻā nallicit tamiḻē

vāḻiya neṭunīr viyanāḷ variyē!



poḻippu :

aṇṇāmaliyār uruvākkiya palkalikkaḻakattiṉ (uṇavu viṭutiyiliruntu ēṟpaṭṭa kalavarattiṉ poruṭṭāy) veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa iḷiñarkaḷ yāvarum oṉṟu kūṭit terut teruvāy ūrvalam vantum, (intiyi nuḻiya viṭamāṭṭōm eṉap palavākiya) uṟuti urikaḷi muḻakkiyum, teḷivāṉa aṟivu cāṉṟa koḷki moḻikaḷi āṅkāṅkuḷḷa cuvarkaḷtōṟum varintum, nīṇṭa kaṟuppu niṟak koṭikaḷip pala vīṭukaḷil ēṟṟi niṟuttiyum, neṭiy toṭarvaṇṭit taṭaṅkaḷil paṭuttu, vaṇṭikaḷit taṭuttum, kalvic ciṟappillāta pulliya moḻiyākiya intiyik kaṟṟuk koḷḷat tuṇṭiya, atit taṭuttu niṟuttaviyalāta pētimi cāṉṟavariṉ aracamippik kaṭintum, kāval niliyaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kāvalarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi paṇintum, ciṟippaṭṭum, uṭal varunta aṭipaṭṭum, neruppup pōlum kotippāṉa urikaḷik kūṟiyum, uṭal uṟuppukaḷ tākkappaṭṭatāl ūṟupaṭṭum, niṉṟa parutta tōḷkaḷi uṭiya māṇavarkaḷukku, attakiya vīra uṇarvu peṟumāṟu uḷḷuyirik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceyta tāḻcciyuṟāta nalla pukaḻi uṭiya tamiḻmoḻiyē, nī viyamāṉatu irukkiṉṟa nāḷvari iruntu vāḻvāyāka!

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

kallāp pulimoḻi intiyi etirttu niṉṟa cellā nallici māṇavar tamakku uḷḷuṇarvu kīṇṭiya tamiḻi vāḻttip pāṭiyatu itu.

“inti nuḻippāl, tamiḻ uṇarvu koḷuttappeṟṟa aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattu māṇavarkaḷ, kalavarattiṉ kāraṇamāka uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉṉi, avarkaḷ aṉivarum oṉṟukūṭi āṅkiruntu terut teruvāy intiyi etirttu muḻakkamiṭṭuk koṇṭu ūrvalam ceṉṟum, intiyi etirkkiṉṟa koḷki varikaḷi āṅkāṅkuḷḷa cuvarkaḷ toṟum eḻutiyum, nīṇṭa kaṟuppuk koṭikaḷip pala vīṭukaḷil ēṟṟiyum, taṅkaḷ etirppit terivikka vēṇṭi, āṅkuḷḷa nīṇṭa toṭarvaṇṭikaḷit taṭuttu niṟuttiyum, kalvic ciṟappillāta pulliya intimoḻiyik kaṟkat tūṇṭiya aṟivaṟṟa aracu āḷumiyik kaṭintum, avaṟṟāl kāvalarkaḷiṉ piṭikkuḷḷākic ciṟippaṭṭum, avarkaḷāl uṭal uṟuppukaḷ ūṟupaṭumāṟu aṭipaṭṭum, akkālum aṭaṅkāmal, neruppup pōlum cuṭukiṉṟa colavakaṅkaḷik kūṟiyum niṉṟa, parutta tōḷkaḷi uṭiya māṇavarkaḷukku avarkaḷ attuṇi vīra uṇarvu peṟumāṟu, avarkaḷiṉ uḷḷuyirik kiḷarcciyuṟat tūṇṭiya, tāḻcciyuṟāta nalla pukaḻiyuṭiya tamiḻ moḻiyē, nī, ulakam uḷḷaḷavum niṉṟu vāḻvāyāka” eṉa vāḻttiyatākum ippāṭal.

aṇṇāmali cey palkalik kaḻakattu - kalvi vaḷḷal aṇṇā maliyāl kaṭṭuvikkap peṟṟa palkalik kaḻakattiṉ.

uṇṇil pōkkiya - uṇṇukiṉṟa illamākiya uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa.

iḷiyōr - iḷimi poruntiya māṇavarkaḷ.

aṇmi - oṉṟukūṭi

teruvalm vantu - terukkaḷ tōṟum ūrvalam vantu.

cūḷuri muḻakki - koḷki uṟutikaḷi urakka olittu.

oḷḷiya koḷki - teḷinta aṟivārnta koḷki moḻikaḷi

miniṟa neṭuṅkoṭi - kaṟuppu niṟamuṭiya nīṇṭakoṭi

paṉmaṉi tūkki - pala vīṭukaḷilum tūkki niṟutti,

kallāp pulimoḻi - kalvic ciṟappillāta puṉmi cāṉṟa moḻi iḻinta moḻi - intimoḻi.

kaṟkeṉattuṇṭiya - kaṟṟuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ eṉat tūṇṭiya.

ollātap pētiyar - inti nuḻivit taṭuttu niṟuttaviyalāta pētimi cāṉṟavar.

aracu kaṭintu - aracik kaṭintu.

kāval maṭam - kāval niliyam.

ēval paṇintu - kaṭṭaḷikkup paṇintu.

valciṟi pōki - valinta ciṟikaḷukkuḷ aṭikkappaṭṭu.

meyyaṭi paṭṭu - uṭal varunta aṭipaṭṭu.

neruppuri - kotikkiṉṟa uṇarvuri.

uṟuppaṟi pōkiya - uṭal uṟuppuc citivu ēṟpaṭṭa

uḷḷuyir kīṇṭa - uḷḷuyiril tōyntirunta tamiḻuṇarvik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceyta

tāḻa nalvicit tamiḻ - eṉṟum etilum tāḻcciyuṟāta ciṟanta pukaḻpeṟṟa tamiḻmoḻi.

neṭunīrviyam - nīṇṭa kaṭaliyuṭiya ulakam. vaṇṭipōl cuḻaṉṟu oṭuvatāl ulaki viyam eṉṟaṉar.

viyanāḷ vari - ulakam nilittirukkiṉṟa nāḷ varikkum.

vaṭa aracu teṉmānilaṅkaḷiṉ mēl kaṭṭāyamākat tiṇikkiṉṟa inti eṉṉum kalvic ciṟappaṟṟa pulliya moḻiyi etirkkat tiṟamiyaṟṟa mānila aracu, atik kaṟkumāṟu māṇavarkaḷit tūṇṭiyatum, atāl veṟuppuṟṟu maṉak kotippaṭinta māṇavar palarum kuṟippāka aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattuk kalvi payiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷ palarum, pōrāṭṭam nikaḻttiya muṟimiyik kūṟi, avvuṇarveḻuccikkuk kāraṇamāyaminta tamiḻ moḻiyi vāḻttik kūṟiyatākum ippāṭal.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum, mutumoḻi vāḻttu eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿56 ⁠kotippuṟu naṟuney





evarkol avarkkē muṉivāṟ ṟummē!

aricāḷ iḻiciṉ puricūḻ neñciṉaṉ;

paṟivāy teṟikkum patta vaccalaṉ

peyarā virukki niṉiviṟ ṟappi

vaṭavarp paṇiyum muṭaveḷ ḷaṟiviṉ⁠ 5

iṭṭa pōkkiṟ kiṭaṟāk kāvalar

cuṭṭuk kiṭattiya centamiḻc ciṟāaṉ

vaḷaṅkūr nāviṉ iḷaṅkō

maruntakat tuḷintuyir tuṟappap puṭintu

kaṟpat tūkkiya cuvaṭi vīci ⁠10

veṟputtōḷil viṟalēṟṟit

teṟuviḻiyil tiyēṟṟic

cennāviṟ ceṟṟattār

uṇṇāvi aṟakkaḻala

orucūliṟ paṉṉuāṟāp ⁠15

puṟṟīyaṟ puṟappāṭṭiṉ

katirkoṉṟa kaḷameṉṉa

etirniṉṟa maramvīḻtti

occumukat taṭakkiyiṉ

kāccitarkkum kaḷiṟaṉṉa⁠ 20

maliyerivāy pīrntaṉṉa

ulineñciṉ veḷiyēṟip

putuppuṉaliṉ kataḻpōkik

kotippuṟu naṟuney yāka

vitirppuṟap paṭippula metirnta ñāṉṟē!⁠ 25



poḻippu :

evartām avarkaḷukku eḻuntu niṟkum kaṭuñciṉatti āṟṟakkūṭiyavar; aracu āḷkiṉṟa iḻimiyum ciṟumiyum koṇṭu, tavaṟāṉa naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyē ārāykiṉṟa uḷḷattiṉaṉum, paṟipōl

muḻaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭṭaḷikaḷit teṟikkiṉṟa vāyiṉaṉum ākiya paktavaccalaṉ eṉṉum mutalamiccaṉ , taṉ pataviyaḻuttattiṉiṉṟum peyarāmal irunta irukkiyiṉ nalattiṉāl, taṉ niṉivil tavaṟiyum, taṉakku mēlarākiya vaṭa nāṭṭiṉarkkup paṇiyum, kōṇalāṉa veḷḷiya aṟiviṉōṭum, tāṉ iṭukiṉṟa kaṭṭaḷikkuc caṟṟum iṭarāta pōkkiṉi uṭiya kāvalarkaḷ,tumukkiyāl cuṭṭukkiṭattiya centamiḻ kākkum iḷiyōṉākiya, vaḷamiyum kūrmiyum uṭiya nāviṉaṉākiya iḷaṅkō eṉṉum māṇavaṉ, maruttuvamaṉiyiṉ kaṇ tuṉpuṟṟu uyir tuṟantāṉāka; ataṉāl aḷāviyeḻunta māṇavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ kaṟpataṟkākak koṇṭu ceṉṟa nūl cuvaṭikaḷi oru puṟattē vīciyeṟintu viṭṭut taṅkaḷuṭiya mali pōlum tōḷkaḷil vīratti ēṟṟik koṇṭu kaṉalkiṉṟa viḻiyil tīyiṉi ēṟṟik koṇṭu, kalvi payilum cevviya nāvil ciṉatti uṭiyavarāka uḷḷuyir aṟavē kaḻaṉṟu veḷiyē kaḻiyumāṟu oru karuppap pēṟṟil pala nūṟu īcalkaḷ puṟappaṭṭu veḷiyēṟutal pōl, katirkaḷip piṭuṅkiyeṟikiṉṟa neṟkaḷam pōlum, etirē niṟkiṉṟa maraṅkaḷiyellām vīḻtti, occiyavāṟu, mukam paṭuttiya parutta kikaḷi nīṭṭik koṇṭu, kāṭṭi aḻikkiṉṟa kaḷiṟukaḷ pōl, erimaliyiṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu citaṟiyaṭikkiṉṟa ulipōṉṟa neñcukaḷi uṭiyavarāki, (aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattiṉiṉṟum) veḷiyēṟip putuveḷḷattiṉ poṅkukiṉṟa ciṉattavarākiya pōkkuṭiyavarākik kotikkiṉṟa nalla neypōl, piṟar naṭukkamuṟa, ak kāvalar paṭikaḷi etirttup pōriṭṭa poḻutil!

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

aṇṇāmali nakarattut tamiḻkākkat tuñciya aracēntiraṉoṭu, kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu maruntakattu uyirtuṟanta iḷaṅkōvaṉip pāṭiyatu.

“kotippuṟu naṟuneyyāka, vitirppuṟap paṭippulam etirnta ñāṉṟē, evarkol avarkkē muṉivāṟṟummē' eṉa iṟuvāyt toṭiyāka vittup poruḷ koḷḷuka.

aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻaka māṇavaṉ aracēntiraṉ kāvalarkaḷiṉ kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu, avviṭattilēyē māyntāṉ; avaṉ naṇpaṉākiya iḷaṅkōvaṉō tumukkic cūṭupaṭṭu, maruttuvamaṉiyil tuṉpuṟak kiṭantu iṟantu pōṉāṉ. avaṉ iṟantu pōṉa ceytiyik kēḷviyuṟṟa māṇavarkaḷ kotippuṭaṉ vīṟukoṇṭeḻuntu, kāvalar paṭiyuṭaṉ pōriṭa etirnta kāṭciyik kūṟuvatākum ippāṭal.

kālamum cūḻalum muṉṉiya pāṭalkaḷuḷ kāṭṭap peṟṟaṉa.

evarkol avarkkē muṉivu āṟṟummē - evartām avark keḻunta cīṟṟatti āṟṟakkūṭiyavarō! evarum illi eṉpatākap poruḷ koḷka.

aricu āḷ... neñciṉaṉ - aracu āḷukiṉṟa iḻimiyum ciṟumiyum koṇṭu, tavaṟāṉa naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyē ārāykiṉṟa uḷattiṉaṉ paktavaccalaṉ eṉṉum mutalamiccaṉ.

paṟivāy teṟikkum - paṟipōlum muḻaṅkukiṉṟa aṭakkamilāta vāy.

peyarā virukki niṉivil tappi - taṉ pataviyaḻuttattiṉ āciyāl ataṉiṉṟu aciyāmal kuntiyirunta irukkiyiṉ nalattiṉāl, taṉ niṉivil tavaṟiyum.

vaṭavarppaṇiyum muṭaveḷḷaṟiviṉ-taṉakku āṭci niliyil mēlarākiya vaṭanāṭṭu naṭuvaṇaraciṉarkkup paṇintu pōkiṉṟa kōṇalāṉa veḷḷiya pullaṟiviṉōṭu.

iṭṭa pōkkiṟku iṭarāk kāvalar- avaṉ iṭukiṉṟa kaṭṭaḷikaḷukkuc caṟṟum māṟāka naṭavāta kāvalpaṭiyiṉar.

cuṭṭukkiṭattiya - tumukkiyāl cuṭṭukkiṭattiya centamiḻc ciṟāaṉ - centamiḻkākkum iḷiyōṉākiya, vaḷaṅkūrnāviṉ iḷaṅkō-vaḷamiyum kūrmiyum uṭiya nāviṉāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṉ eṉṉum māṇavaṉ.

maruntakattu uḷintu uyirtuṟappa - maruttuva maṉiyiṉkaṇ tuṉpuṟakkiṭantu uyir tuṟantataṉāl,

puṭintu - aḷāviyeḻunta māṇavarkaḷ, kaṟpat tūkkiya cuvaṭi vīci - tāṅkaḷ kaṟpataṟkākat tūkkik koṇṭu ceṉṟa nūṟcuvaṭikaḷi orupuṟattē vīciyeṟintuviṭṭu,

veṟput tōḷil viṟavēṟṟi - taṅkaḷiṉ malipōlum tōḷkaḷil vīratti varuvittuk koṇṭu.

teṟuviḻiyil tipēṟṟi - kaṉalkiṉṟa viḻiyil tīyiṉi ēṟṟik koṇṭu. cennāvil ceṟṟattār - taṅkaḷuṭiya kalviyilum cevviya nāvil ciṉatti uṭiyavarāka.

cevviya nāvil ciṉatti uṭiyavarāka eṉṟatu, kotikkiṉṟa kaṭumiyāṉa urikaḷi muḻakkukiṉṟa taṉmiyarāka eṉṟaṟku

uṇṇāvi aṟakkaḻal - uḷ uḷḷa uyir aṟavē kaḻaṉṟu veḷiyēṟumpaṭi, uyir pōkiṉṟavāṟu muḻaṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu. orukuviṟ palnūṟāyp puṟṟiyal puṟappāṭṭiṉ - oru muṟi cūluṟṟu veḷippōnta palanūṟu īcalkaḷ puṟappaṭṭu veḷiyēṟutal pōl.

katirkoṉṟa kaḷameṉṉa - katirkaḷip piṭuṅki eṟikiṉṟa neṟkaḷam pōl.

etirniṉṟa maram vīḻtti - etiratākat teṉpaṭṭu niṟkiṉṟa maraṅkaḷiyellām vīḻttiyavāṟu.

occu mukat taṭakkiyiṉ occiyavāṟu mukattiṉ mēl vitta parutta tumpikkikaḷi nīṭṭik koṇṭu.

kāccitarkkum kaḷiṟaṉṉa - kāṭṭi aḻikkiṉṟa āṇyāṉikaḷip pōl.

maliyeri vāypīrntu aṉṉa-erimaliyiṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu pīcciyaṭittuc citaṟutal pōṉṟa,

uli neñciṉ - kaṉaṟkuḻampiṉ neñcukaḷi uṭiyavarkaḷāki veḷiyēṟi - (aṇṇāmali palkalik kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭat tiṉiṉṟum) veḷiyē pōntu.

putuppuṉaliṉ kaṉal pōki - putu veḷḷattiṉ poṅkukiṉṟa ciṉattavarākiya pōkkuṭiyavarāki;

kotippuṟu naṟuneyyāka - kotikkiṉṟa nalla neyyip pōl.

vitiippuṟa - pārkkiṉṟa piṟar naṭukkamuṟa

paṭippulam etirnta ñāṉṟē - ak kāvalar paṭikaḷi etirttup pōriṭṭa poḻutil

intiyetirppup pōril iḷaṅkōvaṉ kāvalariṉ kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu. maruttuvamaṉiyil uyirtuṟantāṉ eṉṟu kēḷviyuṟṟa aḷavil, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattup payiṉṟa avaṉiṉ orucāli māṇavarkaḷ, kiḷarnteḻuntu, kotippuṟṟu, ak kāvalar paṭiyi etirntu pōyp pōrāṭṭam naṭattiyataṉ cūḻali viḷakkuvatākum ippāṭal.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṉayiyum payilvōr eḻucci eṉtuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿57⁠ emmāṇ makaṉē!





ivaṉiyā reṉkuvī rāyi ṉivaṉē

tāṟali pākar tantuyar poṟāatu

ūṟutaka niṉikkum uyaval yāṉi

kiyukat taṟintu kāltali neṟikku

meyyē pōlum poycēr koṟṟam 5

vaḻaṅkumoḻi uvappa ilaṅkil vaṟumoḻi

pārip puṭaṉṟu nūṟap pukka

kolikōṟ kāvalar kuṟikoḷ eḵkam

atirap paṭarntā rāka

etirtalip paṭṭa emmāṇ makaṉē! 10





poḻippu:

ivaṉ yār eṉa viṉavuvīrāyiṉ, ivaṉē, tam kikaḷil uḷḷa kōṭṭiyiṉāl, taṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttum yāṉippākariṉ tuyarattip poṟuttuk koḷḷāmal taṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttiyavarkkut tāṉum tuṉpam ceyya mikutiyum karutukiṉṟa mataṅ koṇṭa yāṉi, taṉ tumpikkiyi nīṭṭi avari eṭuttuk kīḻē aṟintu, taṉ kālil avar taliyi nacukkic citikkiṉṟa uṇmiyip pōla, poyyē niṟinta aracu, muṉ vaḻakkil uḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyik kuḻappukiṉṟa niliyil ciṟappillāta eḷiya moḻiyākiya inti moḻiyi vaḷarttup parappuvati etirttup pōrāṭi aḻittiṭa māṇavarkaḷ) muyala, (avarkaḷit taṭukkuṟum nōkkattuṭaṉ) koliyuṟac ceyyum kōlik koṇṭa kāvalarkaḷ, kuṟivittavāṟu vēṭṭeḵkam tāṅki, aḵtu atirumpaṭi kaṭṭukkoṇṭē parantu vantārāka, avarkaḷ muṉ etirntupōy iṟantupaṭṭa māṭcimi poruntiya em āṇmakaṉē!

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakamāṇavaṉ iḷaṅkō maruntakattut tuñciṉāṉāka, avaṉi yāreṉa viḷitta ayalārkku avaṉ naṟṟāy colliyatu.

'ivaṉiyār eṉkuvīrāyiṉivaṉē..kolikōṟ kāvalar kuṟikoḷ eḵkum atirap paṭarntārāka, etirtalippaṭṭa em māṇ makaṉē’- eṉṟum.

‘tāṟalip pākar tantuyar poṟāatu ūṟutaka niṉikkum uyaval yāṉi, kiyukat taṟintu kāltali neṟikkum meyyē pōlum-nūṟap pukka’ eṉṟum

koṇṭu kūṭṭip poruḷ koḷḷuka.

ippāṭal muṉṉiya pāṭaliṉ toṭarcci.

kālamum cūḻalum muntiya pāṭalkaḷuḷ kaṇṭukoḷka.

ivaṉiyār eṉkuvīr āyiṉ (itō iṅku iṟanta kiṭakkum) ivaṉ yār eṉṟu viṉavuvīr āyiṉ.

tāṟu ali pākar tam tuyar poṟātu- tāṟu (kōṭṭi - aṅkucam) koṇṭu, kuttit tuṉpuṟuttum yāṉip pākariṉ tuṉpattip poṟuttuk koḷḷāmal.

ūṟutaka niṉikkum uyaval yāṉi - tammit tuṉpuṟuttiyavarkkut tāṉum tuṉpam ceyya mikutiyum niṉikkiṉṟa mataṅkoṇṭa yāṉi.

ki ukattu aṟintu - taṉ tumpikkiyi uyarattu tūkkit (taṉ mēlē ēṟiyirunta pākaṉip paṟṟiyeṭuttut tariyil vīci) aṟintu.

kāl tali teṟikkum - kālāl taliyi nacukkic citikkum.

meyyē pōlum - uṇmiyāṉa naṭimuṟi pōla.

poycēr koṟṟam - poyyik kūṟum poyyarkaḷiyē cērntirukkum iv varacu

poyyik kūṟuvatu, inti paṭittāl naṉmi varum, orumi vaḷarum, vēli kiṭikkum ‘ eṉṟavāṟu eṉka.

vaḻaṅkumoḻi uvappa - vaḻakkil uḷḷa tamiḻ moḻiyik kuḻappic cīraḻikka

ilaṅkil vaṟu moḻi pārippu - ciṟappillāta viḷakkamillāta vaṟiya eḷiya moḻiyākiya intiya moḻiyi vaḷarttup parappuvati

uṭaṉṟu- (māṟupaṭṭu) etirttup pōrāṭi

nūṟappukka- aḻittaṟku (māṇavarkaḷ) muyaṟci ceyya,

koli kōl kāvalar- kolikaḷic ceyyum kōlkaḷi uṭiya kāvalarkaḷ.

kuṟikōḷ eḵkam atira- (māṇavarkaḷ muyaṟciyit taṭukkum nōkkattuṭaṉ) kuṟi vittavāṟu vēṭṭeḵkattit tāṅki, aḵtu atirumpaṭi, cuṭṭuk koṇṭē

paṭarntārāka - parantupaṭṭu vantāraka.

etir talip paṭṭa em māṇ makaṉē - (avvāṟu vanta kāvalarkaḷ muṉṉam) etirntu pōy iṟantu paṭṭa māṭcimi poruntiya em āṇ makaṉē, ivaṉ!

em eṉṟatāl tantiyum uṭaṉiruntatu peṟappaṭṭatu.

pākaṉ taṉṉik kōṭṭiyāl tuṉpuṟuttiyatāl, yāṉi mikka ciṉam koṇṭatu. eṉavē mataṅ koṇṭatu. uṭaṉē taṉ mutukil ēṟiyirunta pākaṉit taṉ tumpikkiyi nīṭṭi, vārit tariyil aṟintu koṉṟu, taliyi mitittut tuvittatu.

atupōl intimoḻi eṉṉum kōṭṭiyāl aṭikkaṭi tuṉpuṟuttiya poyyar cūḻnta aracic, ciṉaṅkoṇṭa māṇavar tuvitteṭukkap pōrkkōlam pūṇṭaṉar - eṉṟu uvamiyi orupuṭip poruttip poruḷ koḷka.

‘aṭarnteḻunta ciṉaṅkoṇṭa māṇavar eḻucciyit taṭuttu niṟuttum poruṭṭuk kāvalarkaḷ, kuṟivittavāṟu vēṭṭeḵkam tāṅkip paṭarntu vantaṉarāka, avar muṉṉam, añcāmal etirntupōy, ak kuṇṭaṭipaṭṭu iṟantu paṭṭa māṭcimi poruntiya em makaṉ, ivaṉ’ eṉṟu avaṉip peṟṟa naṟṟāy, avaṉ yār, ēṉ iṟantāṉ eṉṟu kēṭṭa ayalārkku iḵtu urittaṉaḷ eṉka.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum māṇmakaṉ tuñcal eṉtuṟiyum eṉka. tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿58 ⁠pāṭuka pulavīr

pāṭuka pulavīr pīṭuṭip pāvē!

ēvā ariciṉ eṇṇiyatu tuṇiyum

kāvalar pōrīr nīvir! iṟpukuntu

yāvar tēṭiṉir eṉṉalum; ammiyīr!

mēvaṟu ciṉmoḻi kaṭintu talikkūṭi”⁠ 5

mūvaṟu nāḷā āvaṇat tumiya

cūrpaṭu otiyum eḻucciyum cūṭumā

ūrkoḷ amali aṟintilir pōlum!

kalipuku kaḻakattup pulipuka,

cuvaṭic ciṟāar kavaṭiyi ṉāṭṭa⁠ 10

ūṉum uṟakkamun tuṟantu, pakaliravāk

kāvaṟ paṭuppatuṅ kāṇkilir pōlum!

ivvē maṟukiṉ eḻunta kūṭṭattu

emmōṉ oruvaṉ muṉpal teṟittuk

kuruti pitirak kūrṅkal eṟṟik ⁠15

kaṭitiṉ nuḻintōr ceṅkaṇ viṭali,num

iṟpuṟattuk karantā ṉāka avaṉik

kippaṭat toṭarntēm kāṭṭutir eṉṉa, am

mutiyōḷ naṭukkuṟa veḻuntu,

muṉṟilum akattum maṭiyiṉuṅ kāṭṭi⁠20

oṉṟu maṟikilē meṉṟā ḷāka,

vantōr maṉampeṟak kaḻiya, uṭpukuntu

neṉṉiṟi kurampi oḷiyavuḷ ḷiṟakkiya

puṉṟalic ciṟuvaṉi akavip puṟantukki

aṉṉit tamiḻkkē avalaṅ kaṭintōy ⁠25

niṉṉi makaṉāp peṟṟilēṉ pataṭiyeṉa

iḷantali mukarntu kuruntutōḷ nīvi

velka tamiḻeṉa viṟalpeṟa muḻakki

malka nīrviḻi maṟippac

celkeṉa viṭutta cīrmi niṉintē!⁠30



poḻippu:

pāṭuvīrāka, pulavarīr perumitam tōṉṟum paṭi yāka (itu pōlum) pāṭṭiṉi. itu ceyka eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭār(eṉiṉum) , araciṉariṉ eṇṇam itutāṉ eṉṟu terintu ceyyat tuṇintuviṭukiṉṟa kāvalarkaḷ pōlum, (irukkiṉṟīrkaḷ) nīṅkaḷ. (eṉ) vīṭṭiṟkuḷḷē (kēḷātu) pukuntu, yārit tēṭukiṉṟīrkaḷ. eṉṟu kēṭṭatum, (ataṟku akkāvalarkaḷ). ammiyīr, (tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ kaṇ vantu} paravukiṉṟa ciṟumoḻi(yākiya inti)yi, (avvāṟu parapputali virumpāmal) kaṇṭittu, oṉṟu kūṭi, kaṭanta patiṉeṭṭu nāḷkaḷākak kaṭitterukkaḷ tuāḷipaṭavum, accaṅkoḷḷum (paṭi naṭakkum)āravāraṅkaḷum, pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷum, (ataṉ viḷivākak kāvalartam) tumukkic cūṭukaḷumivvāṟāka ūrē amarkkaḷappaṭum nilikaḷi nīṅkaḷ aṟiyamāṭṭīrkaḷ pōl terikiṟatu.’

‘(eṉavē) kalvipayilum iṭaṅkaḷilellām, puṉmiyāṉa ceyalkaḷ pukuntavāṟu, nūlkaḷi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu payila vēṇṭiya māṇavarkaḷ, eṅkaḷik kavaṭi viḷiyāṭukiṉṟavarkaḷip pōlum āṭṭaṅ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉarāka! ataṉāl, yāṅkaḷ uṇṇum uṇaviyum, uṟakkattiyum aṟavē kiviṭṭavarākap, pakalum iravum (kaṇ viḻittuk) kāval ceyya vēṇṭiyuḷḷatiyum, tāṅkaḷ kāṇavillipōl irukkiṟatu.’

“ip pakkattut teruvil eḻuccikoṇṭa kūṭṭattiṉ (āravārattil) em kāvalaṉ oruvaṉi muṉpal teṟittuviḻavum, ataṉāṉ kuruti pīṟiṭavum, oru kūriya kalli eṭuttu vīciviṭṭu, virintu (ōṭivantu) ippuṟam nuḻintu, civanta kaṇkaḷi uṭiya or iḷantiyaṉ, uṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ uṭpuṟamāka oḷintu koṇṭāṉāka avaṉip piṭippataṟkāka nāṅkaḷ vantuḷḷōm; (avaṉ eṅkē irukkiṟāṉ) eṉṟu kāṭṭuṅkaḷ” eṉavum:

am mutiyavaḷ uṭal naṭukkattuṭaṉ eḻuntu, vīṭṭiṉ muṉpakutiyilum, uḷpuṟattum, (piṉṉuḷḷa) camiyaṟkaṭṭilum, (avarkaḷi aḻittuc ceṉṟu, avaṉ illātatik kāṭṭi), 'eṅkē pōyiruppāṉ eṉṟu oṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭikkak kūṭavilliyē' eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷāka. ataṉāl vantirunta kāvalar, maṉam niṟivuṟṟuc cellalum, (ataṉ piṉṉi avaḷ) vīṭṭiṟku uḷḷākac ceṉṟu, nelli niṟittu vikkum kurampiyiṉ uḷḷē oḷiyac ceyvataṟkāka iṟakkiviṭṭa, pulliya meṉtaliyiṉiyuṭiya ciṟuvaṉik kūvi aḻittu veḷiyē tūkkik koṇarntu, 'tamiḻaṉṉikku vanta tuyarattik kaṭintu niṉṟāyē! niṉṉi yāṉ) makaṉākap peṟāta pataṭiyākiṉēṉē’ eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉatu iḷantaliyiṉi mukarntu mūṅkil pōlum iḷantōḷi nīvik koṭuttut 'tamiḻ velka' eṉṟu vīṟuṇarvuṭaṉ muḻakki, maṇṭukiṉṟa nīr kaṇkaḷi maṟikkumpaṭi, 'nī ceṉṟu viṭu’ eṉṟu viṭutta ciṟanta ceyali eṇṇiyē!

virippu :

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

pōrāṭṭattil īṭupaṭṭuk kāvalar mēl kalleṟinta iḷantiyaṉ oruvaṉ, kāvalarāl turatti varappaṭuṅkāli āṅkuḷḷa illamoṉṟiṉuḷḷē ōṭip pukuntāṉi, mutiyōḷ orutti neṟkurampiyiṉuḷḷē oḷittu vittuk kāttu, akkāvalar veḷiyēṟiya piṉṉi, veḷiyil viṭuttup pōṟṟiya cīrmiyip pārāṭṭiyatu.

'malku nīrviḻi maṟippac celkeṉa viṭutta cīrmi niṉintē, pāṭuka pulavīr pīṭuṭippāvē!’ eṉa iṟuvāyttoṭiyāka iṇittuk kāṭṭuka.

pāṭuka pulavīr pīṭunaṭip pāvē - pāṭuṅkaḷ pulavarkaḷē (ittakiya) perumi miku pāṭalkaḷiyē!

pulavarkaḷ ikkāl pāṭuporuḷāka, vīṇāṉa veṟuñ ceyalkaḷi, veṟṟuc coṟkaḷāl, kaṟpaṉi nayamum vaḷamum iṉṟi, veṟitākappāṭit tam pulamit tiṟaṉip puḻutiyil iṟikkum pulliya ceyalkaḷit tavirttu, ittakiya accamum nakiyum, vīramum vekuḷiyum viyappum uvakiyum aṉpum amitiyum polinta, ilakkiya nayañceṟinta pākkaḷiyē makkaḷ maṉaṅ koḷḷavum nalantuḷumpavum pāṭi makiḻtalum makiḻvittalum vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇṭukiyākap pāṭiyatām ip pāṭal. eṉṉi?

'ēvā ariciṉ eṇṇiyatu tuṇiyum, kāvalar varavum ‘cūrpaṭu ōtiyum eḻucciyum cūṭumā, ūrkoḷ amaliyum eṉpatil, accamum

'cuvaṭicciṟāar kavaṭiyiṉ āṭṭa, ūṉum uṟakkamum tuṟantu, pakaliravāk kāvaṟpaṭuppatum’ eṉpatil nakiyum

‘emmōṉ oruvaṉ muṉpal teṟittuk kuruti pitirak kūrṅkal eṟṟik kaṭitiṉ nuḻintōr ceṅkaṇ viṭali, tum iṟpurattuk karantāṉāka’ eṉpatil vīramum

avaṉik kippaṭat toṭarntōm; kāṭṭutir’ eṉpatil vekuḷiyum;

‘vantōr maṉampeṟak kaḻiya uṭpukuntu neṉṉiṟi kurampi oḷiya uḷḷiṟakkiya puṉṟalic ciṟuvaṉi akavippuṟantukki eṉpatil viyappum, uvakiyum

‘aṉṉit tamiḻkkē avalaṅ kaṭintōy niṉṉi makaṉāp peṟṟilēṉ pataṭiyeṉa, iḷantali mukarntu kuruntu tōḷ nīvi' eṉpatil aṉpum

‘malku nīrviḻi maṟippac celkeṉa viṭutta cīrmi niṉintē' eṉpatil amitiyum;

-iṇintu vantu, eṇ cuviyiyum ippāṭal veḷikkoṇar vati ōrntu uṇarntu makiḻka

-iṉi, ittakiya cuvi nalaṅkoḻikkum pāṭalkaḷip pāṭuvīrkaḷāka eṉpatuṭaṉ, vīra iḷiyōṉik kāval kiyakappaṭuttāmal karantu vittu vāḻtti vaḻiyaṉuppiya mutiyōṉiṉ cīrmiyi emmippōlum nīvirum pāṭuka eṉpatākavum poruḷ koṇṭu pōṟṟuka eṉpatumām.

ēvā ariciṉ ....... kāvalar - kaṭṭaḷiyiṭāmalēyē araciṉariṉ nōkkum pōkkum uṇarntu, atic ceyalākkat tuṇintu niṟkum kāvalar eṉka.

kāvalar pōṟīr- kāvalar pōlvīr eṉpatu, vakaraṅkeṭṭu, iṭiyiṉam valintu, neṭil kuṟukiṟṟu.

pōltal - pōlal-pōṟal (tol-poruḷkaḷakā-3) pōlvīr -pōlīr-pōṟīr ( kavi.9-1, akam 29-12).

iṟpukuntu... eṉṉalum- em illuḷḷē (kēḷātu) pukuntu, yārit tēṭukiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭalum,

mēvuṟu ciṉmoḻi - mēvuṟutaluṟum ciṟiya moḻiyākiya inti, ciṟiya moḻi eṉṟatu, vaḷamiṉmiyum, parappiṉmiyum, ceppamiṉmiyum, ciṟappiṉmiyum karuti eṉka.

kaṭintu- kaṇṭittu, talikkūṭi oṉṟukūṭi.

mūvaṟu nāḷā - patiṉeṭṭu nāḷkaḷāka

āvaṇam tumiya- kaṭitteru tuḷipaṭa..

cūrpaṭu ōti - accam koḷḷumpaṭiyāṉa pōrāṭṭa āravāram. oti ōci yeṉṉuñ col tirintu pērōciyik kuṟittatu.

cūṭu - tumukkic cūṭu amali amarkkaḷappaṭuttum nili.

kali puku kaḻakam - paḷḷikaḷum kallūrikaḷum,

puli puka -pulliya ceyalkaḷ puka kalvikkut tēviyaṟṟa pōrāṭṭam mutaliya āravārac ceyalkaḷ.

cuvaṭic ciṟā ar - māṇavarkaḷ.

kavaṭi-iraṇṭu aṇiyiṉar pōrāṭum oru viḷiyāṭṭu māṇavarkaḷ kāvalarkaḷi etiraṇiyiṉarākak karutik kavaṭiyāṭṭattil viḷiyāṭuvatu pōl āṭṭaṅkāṭṭiṉar eṉpatu.

kāvaṟ paṭuppatu - kāval kāppatu.

ivvē- ivviṭattē maṟuku- teru.

emmōṉ oruvaṉ - em pōlum oru kāvalaṉ.

pitira - veḷippaṭa

ceṅkaṇ viṭali - pōrāṭṭattil toṭarntu īṭupaṭṭatālum, ciṉantu eḻucci koṇṭatālum kaṇkaḷ civantu kāṇappaṭṭa iḷantiyaṉ iḷantipparuvam - piḷḷimikkum iḷimikkum iṭippaṭṭa paruvanili.

iṟpuṟattu - vīṭṭiṉ pakkal.

karantāṉāka - maṟintāṉ āka,

kippaṭat toṭarntēm - kippiṭikkat toṭarntu vantēm.

kāṭṭutir - avaṉ oḷintuḷḷa iṭattik kāṭṭuṅkaḷ.

naṭukkuṟaveḻuntu-mutumiyāl uṭal naṭuṅkiyavāṟu eḻuntu muṉṟil vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟam, akamvīṭṭiṉ uṭpuṟam maṭiyiṉum camiyaṟkaṭṭu uḷḷa, vīṭṭiṉ piṉpuṟam.

maṉam peṟak kaḻiya - maṉam niṟivuṟṟa, veḷiyē cella,

neṉṉiṟi kurampi - nel koṭṭivikka utavum ciṟiya tompi! periya kutir.

puṉṟalic ciṟuvaṉ - iḷantaliyi uṭiya paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ. akavi kūvi aḻittu,

pataṭi - patar pōṉṟavaḷ.

avaṉ pōlum pōrāṭṭa uṇarvuṭiya 9 vīramakaṉip peṟṟilātatāl, taṉṉip pataṭi eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭāṉ eṉka.

kuruntu tōḷ - iḷam mūṅkilip pōlum meṉmiyāṉa tōḷ.

viṟal peṟa -vīṟuṇarvu peṟumpaṭi

malkunīr viḻi maṟippa - malkukiṉṟa kaṇṇir viḻikaḷi maṟikkumpaṭi

celkeṉa viṭutta cīrmi niṉintē - nī ceṉṟuviṭuka eṉṟu viṭutta ciṟanta ceyali eṇṇiyē; (pāṭuka pulavar pīṭuṭip pāvē eṉṟu puṇarttuka)

iḷam uḷḷam oṉṟiṉ tāymoḻippaṟṟiyum, avarkaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiya kāvalarkaḷiṉ koṭumiyiyum, avarkaḷi āṭṭivitta araciṉartam accuṟuttum taṉmiyiyum, pōrāṭṭam naṭanta kālattu, atil īṭupaṭṭārukkup potu makkaḷ. tuṇiyāyiruntu, kāttoḻukiya cīrmiyiyum veḷippaṭutti urippatākum ippāṭal.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum, centamiḻccīrmi eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿59⁠ maruṭki yatuvē!





malimeṉ ṉeñcē! maruṭki yatuvē!

kuliyuṅ kuṭarum vituppuṟ ṟaṉavē!

tamiḻtōy uḷattut tuṇivī teṉkō

vēntumuṉ occiya viṟali teṉkō

vaḷamāṉ tōṉṟal iḷami eṉkō 5



alipuli koṟṟattu viḷivi teṉkō

ulivūṅ kuyiriṉ uvaki eṉkō

āṭuney yoḻuka mucciyi ṉappip

pāṭuveḷ ḷaruvi pāytal pōlum

kaṉṉey muḻukik kaṉalpukun tāṭiya 10



ceñcik kōṭṭattut tēvaṉuārc cemmal

poyyuṭal karukip puṟamāṟi

meyyuṭaṟ koṇṭu miḷirnta ceyalē!





poḻippu:

ticikkum eṉ neñcam! viyappukkuriyatu atu! neñcāṅkuliyum kuṭalum naṭukkamuṟṟaṉa. tamiḻ uṇarvu tōynta uḷḷattiṉ tuṇivu, iḵtu eṉṟu colluvamā? araciṉ muṉ ōṅki niṉṟa veṟṟic ceyal iḵtu eṉṟu colluvamā? perumikkuriya kuṭiyil piṟanta oruvaṉiṉ iḷamic ceyal eṉṟu colluvamā? tuṉpuṟuttalum puṉmic ceyalkaḷum uṭiya araciṉ aṭippaṭiyil viḷinta ceyal iḵtu eṉṟu colluvamā? tuṉpuṟṟa uyiriṉ viṭutali makiḻcci eṉṟu colluvamā? āṭṭiya eḷ neyyi, vaḻintu oḻukumpaṭi taliyiṉ ucciyil tēyttuk koṇṭu, icikkiṉṟa veḷḷiya aruviyil pāytal pōlum, kaṉṉeyyi (peṭrōli) uṭal muḻutum ūṟṟikkoṇṭu, neruppil kuḷittu āṭiya, ceñcik kōṭṭattuḷḷa tēvaṉūric cērnta vīraṉ, kōṭampākkattiṉ muccantiyil, taṉ poyyuṭali karukkik koṇṭu, uyir veḷiyēṟi, meyyāṉa pukaḻuṭali vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭu. oḷiyāy viḷaṅki niṉṟa ceyalē!

virippu:

ip pāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu. ituvum muntiya pāṭal kālattu naṭanta viyappukkuriya oru ceyalip pārāṭṭiyatākum.

ceñcik kōṭṭattut tēvaṉūrc cemmal civaliṅkam eṉṉum iḷiyōṉ, vaṭavarāṭciyiṉ intit tiṇippik kaṭintu kōṭampākkattuk kūṭal maṟukil, kaṉṉey yūṭṭi eripukuntu tuñciya ceyali maruṭkiyuṟap pāṭiyatākum, itu.

“meyyuṭaṟkoṇṭu miḷirnta ceyalē, maruṭkiyatuvē, malimeṉ ṉeñcē eṉṟu iṟutiyum taliyum iṇittup poruḷ koḷka.

malim - maliyum eṉpatu iṭik kuṟintatu- tikikkum.

iḵtu uṇmikol eṉṟu tikittatu neñcam!

maruṭki - viyappu! atu miḷirnta ac ceyal.

kuli - neñcāṅkuli; kuṭar-kuṭal- kaṭippōli.

vituppuṟutal - naṭukkuṟutal.

tamiḻtōy uḷattut tuṇivu - tamiḻ uṇarvu tāṉē vantu poruntiya uḷḷattil tōṉṟiya tuṇivu. eṉṉi? tamiḻil tāṉ tōytalum, taṉṉil tamiḻvantu tōytalum ākiya iraṇṭu viṉikaḷuḷ, muṉṉatu makiḻvuṟutalum, piṉṉatu tuṇivuṟutalum ām eṉka.

vēntu muṉ- araciṉ muṉṉam. -

occiya viṟal- ōṅki niṉṟa veṟṟic ceyal

vaḷamāṉ tōṉṟal - vaḷappamāṉa kuṭiyil tōṉṟiyavaṉ. ivviṭattu vaḷappam perumiyik kuṟittatu.

iḷami eṉkō - iḷamiyiṉ tuṇivāṉa ceyal eṉpamā?

aliyuli koṟṟam makkaḷi alittalum puṉ ceyalkaḷic ceytalum ākiya aracu kollutal urimiyāl koṟṟam āyiṟṟu. vellutal nili veṟṟi āṉatupōla. aracu makkaḷ uyirik kollum urimipeṟṟatu. aracu eṉṉum collum, aricu - aḻittalnilip poruḷik koṇṭatē. iṉi, āḷ eṉṉum vēraṭiyāl āṭciyum, maṉ (nilipēṟu, valimi) eṉṉum vēraṭiyāl maṉṉaṉum varutal kāṇka vēytal (muṭiyaṇital) paṟṟi vēyntaṉ vēntaṉ vēntu eṉavum varutal varalāṟṟup paṭinili vaḷarcci niliyik kuṟikkum col.

ulivu ūṅku uyir - tuṉpuṟṟu nīṅkum uyir.

mucci - tali ucci.

apputal - vārip potital, kiyāl tēyttal. kūṭalmaṟuku - oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa terukkaḷ iṇiyum iṭam. muccanti. nāṟcanti ākiya iṭam.

poyyuṭal iruntu illiyākiviṭum uṭal poyttal illi eṉṟātal.

puṟam māṟi . oriṭattiṉiṉṟu vēṟu iṭattiṟku māṟip pōtal

meyyuṭal- irukkum uṭal pukaḻuṭal,

uyirāṉatu irunta uṭaliṉiṉṟu kaḻaṉṟu, eṉṟum irukkum pukaḻ uṭalil pukutal.

puku pukal (tuḻital, collutal). pukaḻ. uyir uṭal maṟivukkup piṉ, nuḻintu vāḻum uṭal eṉṟum pukaṉṟu koṇṭiruppataṟku uriya taṉmiyāṉum, pukuntu vāḻum taṉmiyāṉum, pukal eṉṉum col uyarvu ciṟappeyti pukaḻ eṉṉum niliciṟappuc collāyt tirintatu. viṉicciṟappu nilipaṟṟi, eḻuttuc ciṟappup pēṟu peṟṟu, nilitta collāṉatu. coṟkaḷ tam tam viṉikaḷukkuriya muṟiyil urumāṟutal coṟpiṟappiyal kōṭpāṭṭil oruvaki eṉka.

miḷirtal - miṉṉutal, oḷirtal, miṉ eṉum vēraṭiyākap piṟanta col.

tamiḻ uṇarvu muṟṟiya uḷḷattiṉ tuṇiviyum, alipuli koṟṟattu viḷiviyum, uyiriṉ kāḻpput taṉmiyil atu taṉṉi iṭam peyarttuk koḷḷum valiviyum, koḷkikkeṉa uyirpeyartal uvaki taruvatu eṉṉum uyirmutirccit taṉmiyiyum ip pāṭaluḷ kaṇṭukoḷka.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum, kaṉṉey āṭal eṉtuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿60 iruntaṉi tamiḻoṭu





puṉiyiṉum pūṭṭu kaḻalaṟap pōkum

miciyiṉum mēṉi tiraṅkalum ākum

ompiṉum vaṉmi kālattu oḻiyum

cāmpiṉum aḻaluṇac cāmpar kaḻiyum

okkal puṟattaḻa vikkal mēluṟṟu 5



mukkalum muṉakalum aṭarntunā pularntu

tikkalum tiṇaṟalum āki uritappik

kakkalum kaḻiccalum ālak kaṇṇavintu

naṉṉaṭi neṟṟi nakarntum kiṭantum

tiṇmamum māṟi nīrmamum koḷḷāp 10



puṟaṅkaṭi vāyiṟ pukaṭṭiya toḻuka

veḷviḻi yēṟi aṟivu veḷiṟi

melamela āvi kaḻivuṟu mēṉāḷ

iruntu pōkiya iṭampārt taḻuum

peruntiraḷ mākkaḷ peṟuvatu miliyeṉum 15



iḻitakik kañciyō makaṉē

taḻalkuḷit tiruntaṉi tamiḻoṭu ciṟantē!





poḻippu:

palavāṟu (pūṭṭiyum aṇintum uṭuttum pūciyum) puṉivu (aḻaku) ceytu vantālum, elumpukaḷāl pūṭṭap peṟṟut tacikaḷāl potiyap peṟṟut tōlāl vēyappeṟṟa ivvuṭalāṉatu, taṉ kaṭṭamippuk kaḻaṉṟu iṟutiyil oṉṟumillāmaṟpōkum. (palavāṟu, palavakiyāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷi) uṇṭu kāttu vantālum, im mēṉiyāṉatu, kāyntu curuṅkip pōkum; itaṉ nalaṉkaḷip palavāṟu pēṇippurantu niṉṟālum, ivvuṭal valimiyāṉatu, kuṟitta kālattil illātu pōyviṭum; avvāṟu valimi oḻintu, oṭuṅkip pōṉa uṭali eri uṇṭu tīrttuc cāmpalākac ceyyiṉum, atuvum oṉṟumillāmal maṇṇil kaḻintupōkum uṟaviṉarkaḷ puṟamāka iruntu aḻavum, iṟuti vikkalāṉatu mēlēṟi niṉṟu, mūccu muṭṭavum, oli muṉakalum mikuntu, nāvāṉatu ularntu pōy, uri tikkalum tiṇaṟuvatum āki, pēca iyalāmal uri ﻿tappippōy, uṇṭatu vāy vaḻiyākavum, cerittatu karuvāy vaḻiyākavum oliyuṭaṉ veḷiyēvara, kaṇkaḷ oḷi avintupōka muṉṉar irunta nallanaṭiyāṉatu taṭumāṟinakarntum, paṭukiṭiyāyk kiṭantum, paruvuṇavu. uṇṭa nili māṟi, nīrma uṇavum koḷḷa viyalāmal, iṉṉoruvar pukaṭṭiya nīrma uṇavum puṟakkaṭi vaḻiyāka oḻuka, veḷḷi viḻi mēlē ēṟavum, aṟivāṉatu maṅkip pōkavum, mella mella āviyāṉatu kaḻikiṉṟa mēli varum nāḷil, tām iṟutiyākap paṭuttiruntu uyir pōkiya iṭattip pārttu aḻukiṉṟa, peruntiraḷāṉa makkaḷ tam vāḻnāḷilum, tām peṟappōkiṉṟatu innikaḻcci tavira vēṟoṉṟumilli eṉum, iḻivu mikunta iv vāḻviṉ muṭivukku añciyō, makaṉē, tīyil kuḷittut tamiḻmoḻiyuṭaṉ eṉṟum ciṟappuṟa cākā niliyil irukkiṉṟāy, nīyē!

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

ituvum muntayi pāṭal kālattu naṭantu viyappukkuriya oru ceyalip pārāṭṭiyatākum.

tīkkuḷiyal ceyta tēvaṉurc cemmal civaliṅkatti uṭal niliyāmi muṉṉiruttip pāṭiyatu. -

puṉiyiṉum aṇikaḷip poruntap pūṭṭiyum mēluṟa aṇintum, uṭukki uṭuttum, maṇa neyyun tuḷum pala pūciyum uṭalip puṉivu (aḻaku ceyyiṉum.

pūṭṭu elumpukaḷālum, tacikaḷālum, nār narampukaḷālum tōlālum pūṭṭappeṟṟa ivvuṭal cērkkiyāṉatu.

kaḻalaṟa aṟak kaḻaṉṟu kaṭṭamippuk kaḻaṉṟu.

miciyiṉum palavakiyāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷi uṇṇiṉum.

mēṇitiraṅkal - uṭalāṉatu kālattāl kāyntu karuṅkutal.

ompiṉum . itaṉ nalaṉkaḷip palavāṟu pēṇip purantālum, vaṉmi valimi;

kālattu oḻiyum- kālattāl illāmaṟ pōkum.

cāmpiṉum - oṭuṅkippōyiṉum

aḻaluṉa - aḻal uṇṇa cāmparcāmpal

okkal - uṟaviṉar. poruntiyiruppavar.

mukkal - kāṟṟu veḷiyēṟa valintu muyalutal. muṉakal - ciṟitu ciṟitākak kāṟṟu veḷiyēṟum oli.

aṭarntu nā pularntu -nā vaṟaṇṭu ularntu pōy.

uri tappi - uri māṟutal - tavaṟutal.

kakkalum kaḻicalum āla- uṇṭatu mēl vāy vaḻiyākavum cerittatu kīḻvāy vaḻiyākavum oliyeḻuppi veḷiyē vara.

kaṇṇavintu - kaṇ oḷi iḻantu.

neṟṟi- taṭumāṟi.

nakarntum kiṭantum- iyaṉṟavaḻi nakarntum, iyalāvaḻik kiṭantum.

tiṇmamum māṟi nīrmamum koḷḷa - parupporuḷ uṇavum tavirntu,nīrma uṇavum koḷḷamuṭiyāmal,



puṟaṅkaṭi vāyil- vāyiṉ puṟattuḷḷa kaṭivāyil

pukaṭṭiyatu oḻuka - piṟarāl nīrma uṇavākap pukaṭṭap peṟṟatu, uḷḷē pōka iyalāmal oḻukumpaṭi

veḷviḻiyēṟi - kaṇkaḷiṉ veḷḷi viḻi mēlēṟi niṟka,

aṟivu veḷiṟi- aṟivuṇarvāṉatu aṟavē illi eṉṟu ākum paṭi illāmaṟ pōka

veḷḷi viḻi mēlēṟik kuttiṭṭu niṟpatum, aṟivu aṟavē illāmaṟ pōvatum, iṟappiṉ iṟuti nikaḻccikaḷ.

melamela āvi kaḻivuṟu mēṉāḷ - mella mella āvi kaḻintu pōkiṉṟa aṭuttu varum iṟuti nāḷ.

iruntu pōkiya iṭam- iṟutiyākac cāppaṭukkiyil paṭuttiruntu uyirviṭum iṭam.

pāttu aḻum-inta iṭattiltāṉē avar paṭuttiruntu uyirviṭṭār eṉṟu uṟaviṉar pārttu aḻukiṉṟa iṭam.

peruntiraḷ mākkaḷ peṟuvatum iliyeṉum- mikka aḷavil tiraṇṭa makkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻvil iṟutiyākap peṟappōvatum im muṭiviviṭṭu vēṟoṉṟu illi eṉṉum.

iḻitakikku iḻivāṉa taṉmikku,

añciyō itupōlum iḻivāṉa vāḻkkikku yāṉum oruppaṭuvatō eṉṟu añciyō,

taḻal kuḷittu - neruppil kuḷippatu pōl mūḻki. tamiḻoṭu ciṟantu iruntaṉi -eṉṟum uḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyuṭaṉ piṟarpōl aḻivuṟātu ciṟappuṟa eṉṟum nī iruntāy kol.

‘ellārum vaḻakkamāka vāḻntu, oru ciṟappum peṟāmal iyalpāka maṟintoḻiyum vāḻkki pōl nāmum vāḻntu maṭiya vēṇṭumō? eṉṟu ēṟpaṭum iḻivukku añciyavaṉākat tamiḻmoḻikkeṉap pōrāṭit tīkkuḷittu māyntaṉaṉ kollō, tēvaṉurc cemmal civaliṅkam? 'avaṉ pukaḻ tamiḻ uḷḷaḷavum ciṟantu niṟkum’ eṉpatākum ippāṭal.

itil oru māntaṉ palavāṟāṉa ciṟappukaḷoṭu vāḻntālum, palavakiyiṉum tuyppuc ceytu iṉpam peṟṟālum iṟutiyil avaṉ vāḻkki, evvakic ciṟappum iṉṟi ellārukkum ēṟṟa iyalpāṉa muṭivē peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē iyalpāka māyntoḻiyum vāḻkkikku oru ciṟapput tēṭuvatu, ataṉi oru koḷkiyōṭu muṭittuk koḷvatē ākum eṉṉum uyar nōkkatti valiyuṟutti inti moḻit tiṇippi etirttut tamiḻmoḻikkeṉat taṉ uyirit tīkkuḷittu māyttuk koṇṭa civaliṅkatti muṉṉiruttip pāṭiyatu ippāṭal.

uyir niliyāmiyum, uṭal aḻivum naṉku pulappaṭuttik kūṟappeṟṟa ip pāṭalil, iṟappuk kālattu nērum cāvuk kuṟikaḷ viḷaṅkak kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

vikkal mēluṟutalum, mūccu muṭṭutalum, nā vaṟaṇṭu pōtalum, uri tikkuvatum, pēcutaṟkut tiṇaṟit tollip paṭutalum, uṇṭatu kakkalum, uḷḷē cerittup pōṉatu taṉṉuṇarviṉṟiyē nīrmalamāyk kaḻivatum, avaṟṟiṉ oliyāravāramum, kaṇkaḷ oḷi kuṉṟuvatum, tappittavaṟi eḻuntāl naṭi taṭumāṟuvatum, eṉavē nakarvatum iyalātaviṭattuk kiṭappatum, paruvuṇavu koḷḷaviyālāmiyum, koṇṭa nīrma uṇavum uḷḷē cellāmal puṟakkaṭi vāyvaḻi veḷiyē oḻukuvatum, veḷḷi viḻi mēlēṟutalum, aṟivu maṟivatum, āvi metumetuvāyk kaḻivatum ākiya cāviṉ paṭippaṭiyāka nikaḻum iṟuti nikaḻccikaḷ naṉku pulappaṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

iviyaṉṟiyum, iyalpāka, ellārkkum vantu nērum vāḻvilum cāvilum eṉṉa ciṟappuḷḷatu? uyiri oru koḷkikkāka iḻappatiltāṉ ciṟappu uḷḷatu eṉṉum, uyar nōkkam iṭam nōkkic cuṭṭappeṟṟatu eṉka.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum,taḻaṟkuḷiyal eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿61. koḷki muḻakkiya kuricil

ivaḷē,

pāriya kūntal puḻuti tuviyat

talitari kiṭattiyum eḻuntumār peṟṟiyum

cevviḻi ukunīr naṉivāy araṟṟiyum

eriyuṇ mūṅkil iṇikoṭi yāki

iṉittuṇi yaṟiyā ivaṉiḷa maṉiyē! 5



ivarē,

tanti koḷki tāmaṟi kallā

aṉṉi aḻuṅkal āṟṟutal aṟiyātu

etirnta cūḻaluk katirnta neñcoṭu

vicittaḻā niṉṟa ivaṉmū makārē! 10



ivaṉē,

vaṭavar tiṇitta vaḷarā maṭamoḻi

kaṭiyat tuṇintu kāṭciyiṉ kāṭṭa

uṭalan tīkkoḷa uyirtīn tamiḻkkeṉak

koḷki muḻakkiya kuricil 15



virukām pākkattu araṅka nātaṉē!





poḻippuri

(ivaḷ yār eṉkuvīrāyiṉ) ivaḷē, paranta kūntal puḻutiyil tōyavum, taliyit tariyil kiṭattiyum, piṉ eḻuntu mārpil aṭittum, civanta viḻikaḷiliruntu ukukkiṉṟa kaṇṇil naṉikiṉṟa vāyāl, araṟṟi aḻutum, eriyāl uṇṇappaṭukiṉṟa mūṅkilip pōl, neyyi ūṟṟik koṇṭu tīyāl paṟṟiyeṟintu uṇṇappaṭukiṉṟa ivaḷ kaṇavaṉmēl iṇintu paṟṟiyirunta koṭiyāki, iṉimēl oru tuṇiyum aṟiyāta ivaṉuṭiya iḷami poruntiya maṉivi; ivaṉarukil niṟkiṉṟa) ivarkaḷ, tantiyiṉatu koḷki neṟiyit tāṅkaḷ aṟiya muṭiyātavarkaḷākat taṅkaḷ aṉṉiyiṉatu aḻukiyiyum, āṟṟukiṉṟa taṉmiyum aṟiyāmal, ēṟpaṭṭa iccūḻalukku atirntupōṉa neñcuṭaṉ, vicittaḻutu niṟkiṉṟa ivaṉuṭiya mūṉṟu makkaḷ (itō, eriyāl uṇṇappaṭṭup piṇamāy kiṭakkiṉṟa) ivaṉ, vaṭavar tiṇitta vaḷarāta maḻuṅkal moḻiyākiya inti moḻiyik kaṭintu taṭuppataṟkut tuṇintu niṉṟu, avvuṇarvik kāṭciyiṉ aḷavil kāṭṭutaṟku uṭalil tīkkoḷuvik koṇṭu, taṉ uyir tīntamiḻukkeṉak koḷkiyi muḻakkiya ciṟanta vīra makaṉākiya, virukāmpākkam eṉṉum ūriṉaṉāṉa araṅkanātaṉ, āvāṉ.

virippu:

ip pāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

ituvum, muntiya pāṭal kālattu naṭanta viyappukkuriya oru ceyalip pārāṭṭiyatākum.

intiyetirppukkāka, tīkkuḷiyal ceyta virukāmpākkattic cērnta araṅkanātaṉip pāṭiyatu.

pāriya - paranta,

puḻuti tuviya - puḻuti tōyumpaṭi tariyil puraḷa,

talitari kiṭattiyum - taliyit tariyil mōti muṭṭi aḻutal,

mārpu eṟṟutal - mārpil kikaḷāl aṭittukkoṇṭu aḻutal,

cevviḻi ukunīrnaṉi vāy araṟṟi - aḻutu civanta viḻikaḷiliruntu ukukkiṉṟa kaṇṇirāl naṉinta vāyāl pulampi aḻutu.

eriyuṇ mūṅkil iṇikoṭi yāki - eriyāl uṇṇappaṭṭu erintu koṇṭirukkiṉṟa mūṅkil mēl iṇintu cuṟṟiya koṭi evvāṟu atuvum karukik kulintu pōkumō, avvāṟu kulintu niṟkiṉṟa koṭi pōṉṟavaḷ āki.

iṉit tuṉi yaṟiyā - iṉimēl, vāḻvut tuṉi yaṟiyāta,

tanti koḷki - taṅkaḷ tantiyiṉ koḷkiyākiya intiyetirppi,

tāmaṟikaḷ lātu - tāṅkaḷ aṟiyāmal,

aḻuṅkal āṟṟutal - aḻukiyit tēṟṟi aṭakkutal,

etirnta cūḻal - ikkāl ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟa cūḻnili.

atirnta neñcoṭu - atircci yaṭinta uḷḷattoṭu,

vicittaḻā niṉṟa - tēmpiyaḻutu niṟkiṉṟa, ivaṉ mūmakār - ivaṉuṭiya mūṉṟu makkaḷ.

vaṭavar tiṇitta vaḷarāmaṭamoḻi - vaṭanāṭṭu āṭciyiṉar teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷ mēl tiṇittirukkum, vaḷarcciyuṟāta mūṭamoḻi aṟivāṟṟal illāta moḻiyākiya intimoḻi,

kaṭiyat tuṇintu - (inti moḻit tiṇippik) kaṇṭittut taṭuttu niṟkat tuṇivu koṇṭu,

kāṭciyiṉ kāṭṭa - taṉ uṇarvik kāṭciyaḷavākac ceyalil kāṭṭutaṟku

uṭalam tīk koḷa- uṭalil kaṉṉey (peṭrōl) ūṟṟik koṇṭu tīppaṟṟumpaṭi ceytu,

uyir tīntamiḻukku eṉa-- taṉ uyir tamiḻukkākavē iruntatu; ikkāl ataṟkut tīṅku vantatāl ataṟkāka atu māyntatu eṉa eṇṇumpaṭi

kuricil - ciṟanta vīra makaṉ; āṇ makaṉ talivaṉ.

virukāmpākkattu araṅkanātaṉ-ceṉṉiyil uḷḷa virukāmpākkam eṉṉum ūril tōṉṟiya araṅkanātaṉ eṉṉum peyariṉaṉ.

taṉ iḷam maṉiviyiyum, mūṉṟu makkaḷiyum poruṭṭeṉa niṉiyātu, intit tiṇippāl tamiḻmoḻikku nērnta tīṅkiyē periteṉa niṉintu, at tīntamiḻmēl tāṉ koṇṭa paṟṟiṉik kāṭṭutaṟkum, intit tiṇippukkut taṉ etirppi veḷippaṭuttutaṟkum, “uṭalit tīyiṉukkum, uyirit tamḻkkum tantēṉ” eṉa eḻuti vittuviṭṭu, taṉmēl kaṉṉeyyi ūṟṟit tī vittuk koṇṭu, māyntu pōṉa, virukāmpākkattic cērnta araṅkanātaṉ eṉṉum vīraṉic cuṟṟiyiruntār kēṭkap pāṭiyatākum, ip pāṭal.

ip pāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum, kaṉṉeyyāṭal eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





62. yāvar kākkum

﻿ emmiyuṅ kāvār; tammiyuṅ kāvār;

kommip paruvattuc ciṉṉāṭ kūṭavil

niṉakkiyāṉ payantaniṉ makāriyuṅ kāvār;

mukilpaṭu muṉṟil mukkoṭi niṟutti



akalneṭuṅ koṟṟattu aracāṇ ṭirunta





5





orumā mutumoḻit tiruviyuṅ kāvār;

vaṭavar ūṉṟiya tamiḻmā naṉṉilam

keṭāatu niṟuttuñ ciṟāriyuṅ kāvār

ceṟṟalar etiriṉ kiḷiyōṭu porutum



aṟṟināḷ tamiḻarip pōlā tiḻinta





10





iṟṟināḷ mākkaḷi aṟintum emiviṭuttu

yāvar kākkumeṉ ṟeṇṇit

tīppukun tāvi tuṟantaṉi nīyē!





poḻippu:

(uṇṭiyum uṟiyuḷuṅ koṭuttuk kākkavēṇṭiya kaṭami koṇṭa nī pōyiṉa. piṉṟi, itō taṉittu niṟkum) eṉṉiyum eṉ ṉōṭu poruntiyavariyum kāttu niṟkār : (aṭimiyum aṟiyāmiyum paṭṭu niṟkum) taṅkaḷiyum kāttuk koḷḷār; iḷamip paruvattiṉ poḻutil, cila nāḷkaḷ niṉṉik kūṭiyataṉ payaṉāka, niṉvaḻi, nāṉ piṟappitta niṉṉuṭiya makkaḷiyum kāttu niṟṟalic ceyyār, vāṉiṉ mukiliṭit tōyumpaṭi, aracamaṉi muṉṟivil mūvēntar nāṭṭiya mūṉṟu koṭikaḷ niṟuttappeṟṟirunta, akaliya nīṇṭa āṭciyamittu, aracāṇṭu koṇṭirunta, ulakiṉ orē periya mutumoḻiyākiya tamiḻccelviyiyum ivarkaḷ kākkum tiṟamuṭiyavarkaḷ allar vaṭanāṭṭiṉar vantu pukuntu peyarāmal niṟkum tamiḻakam ākiya periya nalla nāṭṭiyum, paṭippaṭiyākac citintu pōtaliṉiṉṟu atu keṭāmal kāttaṟkup pōrāṭik koṇṭirukkum (intip pōrāṭṭa) iḷiñarkaḷiyum (avarkaḷukku vantu nērum tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu) kāttu niṟka muyaṟci ceyya māṭṭār. pakivarkaḷ vantu pōrukku etirntu niṟpiṉ, tam iṉattoṭum eḻuntu muṉniṉṟu pōr ceyyum tiṟam vāynta aṉṟiya nāḷil irunta tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷip pōl allāmal, (māṉattum maṟantum tāḻntu aṭimippaṭṭuk kiṭakkum) iṉṟiya nāḷ (tamiḻuṇarvu tirinta) perumi kuṉṟiya makkaḷatu (iṉavuṇarvaṟṟa) taṉmiyi nī aṟintiruntum eṅkaḷi (ivarkaḷiṭiyil) tamiyarāka viṭṭu viṭṭu, vēṟu evar vantu kāppārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇaṅkoṇṭu nī, neyyāl ūṭṭap peṟṟa tīyiṉuḷ pukuntu uṉṉuṭiya uyirit tuṟantaṉiyō?(aṟiyak kūṭavilliyē!)

virippu:

ip pāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu.

ituvum, muṉṉiya pāṭalil pāṭappeṟṟa, tīppukuntu āvi tuṟanta virukampākkattu araṅkanātaṉit tam mūṉṟu makkaḷoṭum cūḻntu niṉṟa maṉivi araṟṟip pāṭiyatu.

emmi - eṉṟatu, avaḷ taṉṉiyum taṉṉic cārnta peṟṟōr mutaliyariyum cuṭṭiyatu.

tammi - eṉṟatu tamiḻiṉa makkaḷi.

kommip paruvam - aḻakum, nalamum, valimiyum cāṉṟa iḷamip paruvam.

ciṉṉāḷ kūṭal - kuṟinta kālamē kūṭiyirunta iḷami vāḻvic cuṭṭiyatu. kuṟinta kāla iḷami vāḻvil.

niṉakku yāṉ payanta niṉmakār- niṉṉāl karuppeṟṟa yāṉ īṉṟaḷitta niṉ mūṉṟu kuḻantikaḷ.

mukilpaṭu muṉṟil - vāṉil tōynta mukiliṭiyil taṭavi niṉṟa aracamaṉi muṉ kōpurattil

mukkoṭi niṟutti -cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyariṉ vil, puli, kayal ākiya mūṉṟu koṭikaḷiyum niṟutti.

akal neṭuṅ koṟṟattu - akaṉṟu nīṇṭirunta āṭci elliyil.

aracāṇṭirunta- āṭci ceytu koṇṭirunta,

orumā mutumoḻit tiru- ulakiṉ oruperum mutumi moḻiyākiya tamiḻ eṉṉum celvi. -

tiruviyum kāvār -. attamiḻ ākiya celviyiyum kāvāta paṟṟum ākkavuṇarvum aṟṟavar.

vaṭavar ūṉṟiya tamiḻmānaṉṉilam -vaṭanāṭṭu āriyar mutaliyar vantu nilippaṭṭuviṭṭa tamiḻ nāṭākiya nalla nilam keṭā atu niṟuttum ciṟār - tamiḻnāṭṭi akappuṟa aḻivukaḷāl citintu pōyviṭāmal, ataṉ perumiyik kāttu niṟkum intip pōrāṭṭa iḷiñar.

intip pōrāṭṭak kālattu itu naṭantamiyālum, ap pōrāṭṭatuṭaṉ taṉ tāṉum oruvaṉāy niṉṟu, ivaṉ uyirtuṟantatu nikaḻntamiyālum, īṇṭuc ciṟār eṉṟatu intip pōrāṭṭa iḷiñarik kuṟittatu.

ceṟṟalar etiriṉ kiḷiyoṭu porutum aṟṟināḷ tamiḻar - pakivarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēṟ pōr toṭuppārākil, tamiḻaruḷ nilattāl vēṟupaṭṭirunta aṉittuk kiḷiyiṉarum oṉṟāy iṇintu, pakiyi etirttup pōrāṭum iṉavuṇarvu vāynta muṉṉāḷiya tamiḻar

pōlātu iḻinta- (avarkaḷip) pōl allāmal aṟiyāmiyālum aṭimit taṉmiyālum tāḻntu iḻintu niṟkiṉṟa.

iṟṟināḷmākkaḷ-iṉṟiya nāḷil uḷḷa, iṉanalaṉum, maṉanalaṉum māṉanalaṉum tirinta makkaḷip pōṉṟa mākkaḷ - vilaṅkukaḷ.

aṟintum - tam moḻi iṉa, nāṭṭu nalaṉkaḷiyē kāttuk koḷḷāmal aṭimiyākiviṭṭa ivarkaḷā pōrāṭṭa vīraṉ oruvaṉiṉ peṇṭu piḷḷikaḷik kākkap pōkiṟārkaḷ. ; māṭṭārkaḷ’ - eṉpati nī aṟintu koṇṭiruntum.

emi viṭuttu - eṅkaḷit tamiyarāka ivarkaḷiṭiyil viṭṭu viṭṭu.

yāvar kākkumeṉ ṟeṇṇi - ivarkaḷ iṉattuk kuriyavarākiya eṅkaḷi, ivarkaḷiyaṉṟi, vēṟu enta iṉattavar vantu kākkap pōkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu etirpārttu.

tippukuntu āvi tuṟantaṉi nīyē - kaṉṉeyyāl koḷuvap peṟṟa tīyiṉuḷ niṉṉuṭali aḻiyac ceytu, āviyit tuṟantu koṇṭiṉi, nīyē!

tamiḻi maṭṭumē kākkap pōrāṭiya nī uṉṉiyē nampiyirukkum eṅkaḷit taṅkaḷiyē kāttuk koḷḷa iyalāta ivviṉattāriṭiyil tamittirukka viṭṭuviṭṭu, niṉ āviyi erippukuntu māyttuk koṇṭaṉiyē col, iṉimēl eṅkaḷik kāppavar yāvar?’ eṉṟu taṉ maṉa iṟukkatti aviḻttu avaṉ maṉivi araṟṟi aḻutāḷ’ eṉpatākum ippāṭal.

ippāṭal muṉṉatu tiṇiyum tuṟiyum ām eṉka.

tiṇiyum tuṟiyum putiyaṉa.





﻿63 aṉṉiyum maṟantāḷ

aṉṉiyum maṟantāḷ; āyamum maṟantāḷ;

iṉṉa paṇpiṉaṉ iṉiyaru ḷāḷaṉ

eṉṟivi tērātu tantiyum maṟantāḷ;

maṟṟivaḷ viḻumattu maṉṟukoḷa niṉṟa

ciṟṟil āṭuñ ciṟumiyum maṟantāḷ; 5



kiḷikiḷi vanta vuṟavu meṇṇātu

iḷiyōṉ pāṅkiṉ viḷivum orātu

nerunal pāvikku aṟuvi uṭuttuc

ciṟuveṇ muttattut toṭiyal cāṟṟi

maṟukiṉ āṭiya kuṟunutal 10



paṟantiya ceṉṟatu yārkoḷap payiṉṟē!





poḻippu:

(maṟattaṟkē iyalātavaḷākiya taṉṉip peṟṟa) aṉṉiyiyum maṟantāḷ; (taṉṉoṭu cērntu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟa taṉ akaviyotta) tōḻikaḷiyum maṟantāḷ puṟavulakil oruvaṉi iṉṉa paṇpi uṭiyavaṉ, iṉṉa aruḷāṇmi uṭiyavaṉ eṉum im maṉaviyal kūṟukaḷit tēṟavaṟiyāta ivaḷ, (avvāṟu tēṟi ivaḷukkup poruntiyavaṉi aṟivikkum tiṟaṉuḷḷa) taṉ tantiyiyum maṟantu pōṉāḷ maṟṟum, ivaḷ taṉ vāḻvi amittuk koḷḷum perumiyiṉālum taṉmiyiṉālum, aṭuttuḷḷa taṉ maṇavāḻvi amittuk koḷvataṟkut toṭarntu niṟkiṉṟavaḷum, ikkāl ciṟṟil kaṭṭi viḷiyāṭik koṇṭiruppavaḷum ākiya taṉ taṅkiyākiya ciṟumiyiyum ivaḷ maṟantu viṭṭāḷ.

vaḻivaḻiyāka vanta uṟaviṉarkaḷiṉ toṭarpukaḷiyum eṇṇāmal ivaḷ piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟa iḷiyavaṉiṉ uṟavāl toṭarappōkum viḷivukaḷiyum ārāyntu pārāmal, nēṟṟu (eṉṉum kāla niliyil), (tāṉ vittukkoṇṭu viḷiyāṭiya ) marappāvikku, āṭikaḷi uṭuttiyum, ciṟiya veṇmuttukkaḷāl āṉa māliyiṉic cāṟṟiyum teruvil āṭikkoṇṭirunta ciṟiya neṟṟiyi uṭiya ivaḷ, (veḷiyūrkkut taṉ kātalaṉuṭaṉ) paṟantu ceṉṟatu yār payiṟṟuvitta payiṟciyiṉāl? virippu :

ippāṭal akattuṟiyic cārntatu

tāṉ aṉpuṭaṉ vaḷartta makaḷ, taṉ aṉṉiyiyum taṉṉōṭu otta āyattiyum, taṉṉip pēṇip purakkum tantiyiyum, taṉ uṭaṉ piṟanta taṅkiyiyum, taṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷi maṟantum, eṇṇip pārāmalum, tāṉ kātalittu uṭaṉpōkiya iḷiñaṉāl varum viḷivukaḷiyum eṇṇippārāmal viḷiyāṭik koṇṭirukkum mikkiḷam paruvattiṉaḷākiya ivaḷ vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip pōṉatu, yār payiṟṟuvitta tuṇiviṉāl eṉṟu cevilittāy pulampi varuntiyatākum ippāṭal.

aṉṉiyum maṟantāḷ - maṟappataṟkum ariyaḷākiya taṉṉip peṟṟa tāyiyum maṟantāḷ.

‘ummi ciṟappum arumiyum uṇarttiyatu.

āyamum maṟantāḷ-taṉṉōṭotta makaḷir kūṭṭattiyum maṟantāḷ.

aṉṉik kaṭuttapaṭi taṉṉoṭu neruṅkiyiruntu paḻakiyum payiṉṟum āṭiyum makiḻpavar avaḷotta tōḻiyarākaliṉ avariyum maṟantāḷ eṉpatu viyappukkuriyatu.

iṉṉa paṇpiṉaṉ, iṇiyaruḷāḷaṉ eṉṟivi tērātu - iṉṉa paṇpiṉi uṭiyavaṉ, inta aḷaviṉ aruḷāḷaṉ eṉṉum ivviyaṟkip paṇpukaḷit tēraviyalātavaḷāki,

peṇ orutti, tāṉ maṇakkappōvāṉippaṟṟi aṟintu koḷvataṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta kuṇaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu ; avi avaṉatu paṇpum aruḷ taṉmiyumām eṉka.

paṇpu avaṉ avaḷi vittu naṭattum illaṟattiṟkum, aruḷ, avaḷpāl avaṉ koḷḷum ārvattiṟkum akkaṟikkum aṭippaṭiyām eṉka.

iṉi, ivviraṇṭu paṇpukaḷiyum ulakiyalaṟivu cāṉṟa mutiyōrē aṉṟi avaṉ puṟaveḻilāl mayaṅkum kātaṟpeṇ attuṇic ciṟappākat tērtaṟku iyalātatākiyiṉ tērātu’ eṉṟāḷ.

iṉi avvāṟu tērtaṟku uriyavaruḷ avaḷ tantiyē takkavarākaliṉ, ak karuttiṉit toṭarntu attaku tantiyum maṟantāḷeṉṟāḷ eṉka.

maṟṟivaḷ viḻumattu, viḻumam - perumi, maṟṟu, ivaḷ perumiyiṉām; taṉmiyiṉālum ciṟappu, naṉmi eṉum palaporuḷ paṭum oru col. maṉṟukoḷa niṉṟa ciṟṟil āṭum ciṟumi tirumaṇattiṟku aṇiyamāki niṟkiṉṟa, ciṟṟil viḷiyāṭum taṅkiyākiya ciṟumi.

akkiyiṉ tirumaṇam nikaḻntu, ataṉ perumiyiṉatum naṉmiyiṉatum aṭippaṭiyil taṅkikkum maṇam vantu kūṭum taṉmi īṇṭuk kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.

perumi - akki maṇantu koṇṭa maṇamakaṉiṉ taṉmiyāl varum kuṭumpac ciṟappum, avaṉukkotta takutiyil taṅkiyiyum maṇampēca varum maṇamakaṉatu ciṟappum

naṉmi- akkiyi maṇantu koṇṭavaṉāl, avaḷ taṅkiyiṉ tirumaṇattiṟku varum kuṭumpavutavi ; atu maṇaviṉittuṇiyum poruḷutaviyum ākalām eṉka.

kiḷikiḷi vanta uṟavu - marapāṉ toṭarntu varum kuṭumpa uṟavu.

kuṭumpa uṟavu mēlum toṭarvāṉ vēṇṭi, ivaḷi maṉaṅkoṭukkavum, maṇantu koḷḷavum takutiyuṭiya uṟaviṉarkaḷ.

avarkaḷiyum eṇṇātu, avarkaḷ ivaḷi maṇantu koḷḷaviyalātu pōṉatāl varum ēmāṟṟattiyum a'tāl varum pakiyiyum ciṟitum uṇarāmal, ayalāṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ, avaṉit taṉ viruppamāka maṇantu koḷḷa vēṇṭi vēṟṟur pōkiṉaḷē eṉṟu pulampiṉāḷ eṉka.

iḷiyōṉ pāṅkiṉ viḷivum orātu - ivaḷi uṭaṉ pōkkic ceṉṟa iḷiñaṉākiya ivaḷ kātalaṉiṉ putiya kūṭṭuṟavāl varum palavakiyāṉa etirviḷivukaḷiyum ārāyntu pārāmal,

putiya uṟavāl, ivaḷi muṉkūṭṭi aṟiyāta pukkaka uṟaviṉarkaḷālum, ivaḷ peṟṟōriṉ, uṟṟāriṉ perumiyaṟiyāta putuvuṟavuk kaṇavaṉālum, aṟiyāmiyum matippiṉmiyum kalantu ēṟpaṭappōkum etirviḷivukaḷ eṉka.

nerunal.-nēṟṟu kāla aṇminōkki, ulakiyal vaḻakkil nēṟṟu eṉṟāḷ.

pāvikku - marappāvikku.

aṟuvi uṭuttu - āṭiyi uṭutti.

ciṟuveṇ muttattut toṭiyal - ciṟiya veḷḷiya muttukaḷāl ākiya māli.



cāṟṟi - cāṟṟuvittu, aṇivittu. maṟukiṉ āṭiya kuṟunutal - teruvil iṟaṅki viḷiyāṭiya ciṟiya nutaliyuṭiya ip peṇ.

nerunal poḻutil, marappāvikku āṭiyuṭuttu muttu māli aṇivittu viḷiyāṭiya ic ciṟupeṇ, avviḷiyāṭṭip pōlavē ivvuṭaṉ pōkkiyum, ivaḷ kaṇavaṉiyum karuti, mēṟkoṇṭāḷ pōlum eṉṟu pulampiṉāḷ eṉka.

paṟantiya ceṉṟatu - vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟic ceṉṟatik kūṭṭiviṭṭuc ciṟaku muḷitta paṟavi paṟantu ceṉṟatupōl eṉṟu karutik kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. iya-virivu aci

yār koḷap payiṉṟu - yār payilvittup payiṉṟu koṇṭu.

tāṉ vaḷartta arumi makaḷ, tāṉ peṟṟa aṉṉi tantiyariyum uṟṟār uṟaviṉariyum ittuṇi virivil tuṟantum maṟantum, putiya uṟavāṉa kātal iḷiñaṉuṭaṉ vēṟṟuār ceṉṟāḷē, im maṉattuṇivi varuvittavarum payilvittavarum yārō aṟikilēṉē eṉa alamantu pulampiṉāḷ cevili eṉka.

ippāṭal pālit tiṇiyum cevili pulampal eṉṉum tuṟiyum eṉka.





64 aṟitikol maruntē

﻿ maruttu vōṉē! maruttu vōṉē!

tiruttāk koṭunōyk kiyikan tōrik

karuttukat taṇikkum maruttu vōṉē!

poṟippula ṉintuñ ceṟitta vāki

neñcu putittu nañkari kuyilvōr 5



añcu viṉikkuyir añcā tuḻalvōr

naṉṟi koṉṟavar naṉṉaṭip pōliyar

eṉṟivark kaṟitikol maruntē

kaṉṟiya neñcōr kaḷippuṟa vurimē!





poḻippu :

maruttuvōṉē! maruttuvōṉē! nalappaṭuttaviyalāta koṭiya nōyiṉāl, palarālum kiviṭappeṟṟavarkaḷik karuttuṭaṉ (kavaṉittut taṇittu nalamākkum (āṟṟal vāynta maruttuvōṉē! aintu pulaṉcārtta poṟikaḷum nalla muḻuttiṟaṉuṭaṉ iyaṅkukiṉṟa niliyil, tam (uṇmi) neñcip putittuk koṇṭu, nañcu tōynta iṉimi urikaḷip pēcuvōr, ulakam añcukiṉṟa ceyalkaḷi añcāmal ceytu tirivōr ceyta naṉmikaḷik koṉṟavarkaḷ, puṟattē nalla naṭiyiṉarākiya akattup pōliyar - eṉṉum ivarkaḷi nalappaṭuttum maruntiṉi aṟivāyō? aṟiyiṉ, (ivarkaḷiṉ koṭiya nilikaḷik kaṇṭu) maṉam nontupōy iruppavar, makiḻveytumpaṭi urippāyāka.

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyiccārntatu.

ulakiyalil, nalla uṭal nalaṉkaḷ cārntavarkaḷāki, uḷḷattiṉ nalluṇarvukaḷip putittuk koṇṭu, nañcupōlum tīmi payakkukiṉṟa urikaḷi iṉimiyākap pēcuvōrkaḷālum, makkaḷ añcukiṉṟa koṭiya ceyalkaḷit tuḷiyum añcāmal ceyvōrkaḷālum piṟar ceyta utavikaḷukkuriya naṉṟiyuṇarvu illātavarkaḷālum veḷiyil nalla naṭimuṟikaḷi uṭiyavarkaḷāki, akattē vēṟupaṭṭa uṇarvukaḷik koṇṭa pōli māntarkaḷālum uḷḷam nontu varuntiya oruvar, piṟarāl nalamākka muṭiyāmal kiviṭappaṭṭa koṭiya nōyāḷarkaḷiyum kuṇappaṭuttukiṉṟa tiṟaṉvāynta maruttuvariṭam, mēṟkaṇṭārik kuṇappaṭuttum maruntum uḷatō ? uṇṭāyiṉ terivikka vēṇṭik koṇṭatākum. ippāṭal.

maruttuvōṉē maruttuvōṉē - eṉa irumuṟi aṭukkiyatu, iṉṟiyamiyāt tēviyiyum, maruttuvariṉ perumiyiyum uṇarttiyatu.

tiruttāk koṭunōy - evarālum nīkkaviyalāta koṭiya nōyiṉāl,

kiyikantōri ;kiviṭṭavarkaḷi, kiviṭappaṭutal- nōy kaṭumiyāṉatāl ati nalamākka iyalāmal nōyāḷikaḷik kiviṭṭu viṭutal.

karuttukat taṇikkum - karuttu mikumpaṭi kavaṉittu annōyit taṇikkum.

maruttuvōṉē eṉṟu mūṉṟām muṟiyum viḷittatu iṉi kūṟappōkum karuttiyum kavaṉikkavēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇṭutal poruḷik kuṟittatu eṉka.

poṟippulaṉ aintum ceṟittavāki - poṟikaḷ mēl niṉṟu iyaṅkum aintu pulaṉaṟi nilikaḷum ceṟivuṭaṉ iruntu paḻutiṉṟi iyaṅkuvaṉa vāki,

neñcu putittu nañcuri kuyilvār- neñcu kaḷaṉāka niṉṟu iyaṅkum maṉac cāṉṟiṉip putittuviṭṭu, vāyil nañcupōl tīmi ceyyum urikaḷik kuyilpōl iṉimiyākak kūṟuvār. kuyilutaliṉimiyākap pēcutal puṇarcciyil ukaram keṭṭatu.

añcu viṉikku - makkaḷ añcattakka ceyalkaḷukku.

uyir añcātu uḻalvōr - uyirkku varun tuṉpattiṟku añcāmal cuḻaṉṟu iyaṅkuvōr.

naṉṟikoṉṟavar- ceyta utavikaḷi niṉittup pārāmal avaṟṟic ceytavarkaḷukkē tīṅku ceypavar.

naṉṉaṭip pōliyar - puṟattē nalla kuṇamuḷḷavarkaḷpōl naṭantu akattē vēṟupaṭum pōli māntarkaḷ.

eṉṟu ivarkku - eṉṉumpaṭiyāṉa ippaṭippaṭṭa uḷanōy koṇṭavarkaḷukku

aṟitikol maruntē - aṟivāy kol takka maruntiṉiyē. kol acinili iṉi kol maruntē eṉak koṇṭu, avvāṟu maruntillāviṭiṉum, avarkaḷik koṉṟaḻikkum maruntiyākilum aṟiviyō eṉṟu acikkum poruḷkoṇṭu urikkalām eṉka.

kaṉṟiya neñcōr-mēṟkūṟiya nālvaki makkaḷ eṉṉun tōṟṟattut tiripuḷḷam koṇṭavarkaḷik kaṇṭu koṇṭu nontu kacintupōṉa uḷḷamuṭiyavarkaḷ.

kaḷippuṟa urimē - makiḻvuṟumpaṭi kūṟuvāyāka

potuvāṉa nalla māntarkaḷukkuriya vakiyil nōyppaṭāta uṟuppukaḷik koṇṭiruppiṉum, nañcuḷḷam uṭiyavarkaḷāka iṉiya urikaḷip pēcupavarkaḷiyum, tīyaṉavaṟṟi añcāmal ceypavarkaḷiyum, naṉmi ceytārkkum tīṅku ceyyum naṉṟiyaṟṟavarkaḷiyum, nallavarkaḷ pōl naṭakkum pōli māntarkaḷiyum. tiruttuvataṟku ētēṉum maruntu uṇṭō, tīrāmal kiviṭappaṭṭa nōyāḷarkaḷiyum mikkak karuttuṭaṉ kavaṉittu avarkaḷiṉ nōykaḷit tīrkkiṉṟa maruttuvaṉē avvāṟu maruntu uḷatāyiṉ, anta māntat tiripuḷḷavarkaḷukku ataṉik koṭuttu avarkaḷit tirutti, avarkaḷik kaṇṭu uḷḷam varuntukiṉṟavarkaḷ makiḻumāṟu, avarkaḷi nallavarkaḷākac ceyvāyāka eṉṟapaṭi,

ippāṭal potuviyal eṉṉum tiṇiyum, mutumoḻik kāñci eṉṉumtuṟiyum eṉka.





65 kurakkiṉ kaḷinaṭam

﻿ kāciṟ killi; kantaṟ killi;

mūcunar iḷaṅki koṭṭi muḻakki

akaṭu valikkum ukunakik killi;

takavup paṭuttun taṉṉik kōṟkut

tāṇṭiyum cuḻaṉṟum vēṇṭiya āṭuṅ 5



kāṇṭaku kurakkiṉ kaḷinaṭam pōla

eṉṉik kallā laṉṉi

naṉṉaya vurikku maṉṉātu neñcē!





poḻippu :

kācukkākavum illi; kantal tuṇikaḷukkākavum illi (pārkkak) kūṭuvōr iḷiya kikaḷ taṭṭi varavēṟṟu, makiḻcciyāl pēroli eḻuppi vayiṟu valikka, ukukkiṉṟa naki muḻakkattiṟkākavum illi : taṉṉi neṟippaṭutti iyakkukiṉṟa taṉ talivaṉiṉ kiyiluḷḷa kōlukku icintē, atit tāṇṭavum cuḻalavum avaṉ virumpiya vakiyil ellām āṭukiṉṟa, kāṇattakunta kuraṅkiṉ kaḷippāṉa naṭaṉattip pōla, eṉ talivaṉukku allāmal aṉṉiyiṉatu aṉpāṉa mellurikaḷukkup poruntuvatilli eṉ neñcam.

virippu:

ippāṭal akattuṟiyic cārntatu.

talivaṉ mītu ārāk kātal koṇṭa talivi taṉkātalit taṉ neruṅkiya tōḻikkuk kuṟippāka uṇarttiyatākum ippāṭal.

teruvil naṭanta kuraṅkāṭṭattit taṉ illattiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭu kaḷitta talivi, arukirunta tōḻiyiṭam, akkuraṅkik kāṭṭi, atu pōlvatē ākum taṉ niliyum eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ talivaṉiṭam taṉakku irukkum īṭupāṭṭip pulappaṭuttik kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.

īṇṭu it teruvilāṭum ikkuraṅku, kācukkākavum āṭavilli, vēṭikki pārkkiṉṟavarkaḷ koṭukkum kantal tuṇikaḷukkākavum āṭavilli. allatu aṅkuk kūṭiyuḷḷa ciṟuvarkaḷ kuraṅkiṉ vēṭikkikaḷip pārttuk kikaḷit taṭṭi makiḻntu āravāram ceytu eḻuppukiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ cirippolikkākavum āṭavilli. taṉṉi avvāṟellām paḻakki, āṭṭuvikkiṉṟa taṉ talivaṉiṭam irukkiṟa kikkōlukkākavē atit tāṇṭiyum ati vittuk koṇṭu cuḻaṉṟum avaṉ virumpiya vāṟellām ellārum kāṇattakunta makiḻvāṉa naṭaṉatti āṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.

tāṉum appaṭittāṉ; taṉ talivaṉ taṉṉi aṉpāl paḻakkik kātal eṉṉum uṇarvāl āṭṭuvikkiṉṟāṉ. eṉavē taṉ tāy taṉṉiṭam nayamāka urikkum piṟa urikaḷukkō, avarkaḷ taṉakku uṇavum uṭiyum tantu makiḻntu purakkiṉṟa aṉpuṇarvukkō tāṉ kaṭṭuppaṭaviyalavilli. eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam taṉ talivaṉiṭam taṉakkuḷḷa āḻamāṉa kātali uṇarttik kūṟiyatākum ippāṭal.

kāciṟkilli kantaṟku illi - kuraṅkāṭṭiyiṉ kiyiluḷḷa kuraṅku makkaḷ koṭukkum kācukkō, allatu avarkaḷ koṭukkum kantal tuṇikkō āṭuvatilli.

atupōl tāṉum, taṉ peṟṟōrō allatu taṉṉi maṇam ceytu koḷḷa virumpuvāṉ vēṇṭi varuvōṉō taṉakkup poruḷ nalamum uṭinalamum koṭuppiṉum, taṉ uḷḷam avaṟṟi nāṭi avarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu iyaṅkuvatilli eṉṟāḷ eṉka.

mūkanar. illi - kuraṅkāṭṭattip pārkkak kūṭiyiruppavarkaḷuḷ iḷiyōr tam kikaḷi makiḻcciyāl koṭṭi, vayiṟukaḷ valikkumpaṭi cirikkiṉṟa pērolikkākavum, akkuraṅku makiḻntō araṇṭō āṭavilli.

atupōl, taṉ peṟṟōrum maṟṟōrum taṉ akaviyiyum aḻakiyum, naṭimuṟikaḷiyum pārttu makiḻcciyālō aṉṟi ikaḻcciyālō, orupuṟam pārāṭṭavum-maṟupuṟam avar tūṟṟavum ceyyiṉum, avvāravāraṅkaḷakkākavum tāṉ avarkaḷ vaḻi niṟkavum iyalātu.

takavup paṭuttum taṉṉik kōṟku - ataṉip payiṟṟi iyakkum kuraṅkāṭṭiyākiya ataṉ talivaṉiṉ kiyil uḷḷa kōlukkuicintē

tāṇṭiyum.āṭum - tāṇṭavum cuḻalavum āka avaṉ virumpiyapaṭiyellām āṭukiṉṟa.

kāṇṭaku kurakkiṉ kaḷinaṭam pōla - kāṇpataṟkukanta kuraṅkiṉatu makiḻvāṉa naṭaṉattip pōla.

eṉ jakku allāl, aṉṉi naṉṉaya urikku - eṉ talivaṉiṉ aṉpurikku allāmal, eṉ tāyiṉ naṉmiyum nayaṉmiyum cāṉṟa urikku. maṉṉātu neñcu - poruntātu eṉ neñcam.

kuraṅkāṉatu, taṉṉi āṭṭuvikkum taṉ talivaṉatu kiyiluḷḷa kōlukku iyintu naṭappatip pōl, yāṉum eṉ talivaṉatu kātal uṇarvukkuk kaṭṭuṇṭu naṭakkiṉṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ.

kuraṅkit takavup paṭuttuvatu avaṉatu kōl, atupōl taliviyit takavuppaṭuttuvatu avaṉatu kātalaṉpu takavup paṭuttutal, uṭaṉpaṭṭu oppa naṭakkac ceytal.

kōliṉ poruṭṭāka ak kuraṅku tāṇṭiyum cuḻaṉṟum kaḷinaṭam payilvatu pōlat taliviyum talivaṉiṉ kātaṟporuṭṭāka, illantāṇṭip puṟattē ceṉṟum, aṉpāl avaṉic cuḻala (cuṟṟi) vantum, kātal uṇarvāl kaḷippuṟak kaḷavu āṭiyum varukiṉṟatit talivi tōḻikkuk kuṟippāy uṇarttiṉāḷ eṉka.

‘aṉṉiyiṉ uri nallatu nayamāṉatu; eṉiṉum eṉ uḷḷattē atu poruntuvatu illi. kuraṅku taṉṉic cuṟṟiyiruppavariṉ makiḻvum pārāṭṭum nirampiya cirippolikku iyiyātu taṉ talivaṉiṉ kikkōlukkē āṭuvatupōl, nāṉum eṉ talivaṉiṉ kātal uṇarvākiya kōlukkē ivvāṟellām naṭakka vēṇṭi uḷḷatu, eṉṟāḷ talivi.

kuraṅkāṭṭattip pārttuk kūṭṭattil uḷḷa iḷavōr kikoṭṭi nakippatu pōlat taṉ kātal-kaḷavu naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyum taṅkaḷiṉ aṉpuṇarvi aṟiyāta mūṭar iḻittum paḻittum avar tuāṟṟuvatik kuṟippākavum, āṉālum atit tāṉ poruṭ paṭuttātati uṟutiyākavum uṇarttiṉāḷ talivi eṉka.

‘maṉṉātu neñcam’ eṉṟa taṉ neñcam talivaṉiṉ aṉpāṉa kātaluṇarvukku maṭṭumē allāmal, piṟar evaruṭiya evvuriyāṉālum ataṟkup poruntavum poruntātu, nilippāṭum eytātu eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.

iṉi kuraṅku taṉ peṟṟōriyum piṟantaviṭattiyum viṭṭu veḷiyēṟit taṉ talivaṉiṉ vāḻvōṭu porunti vāḻvatu pōl, tāṉum taṉ peṟṟōriyum piṟantaviṭattiyum viṭṭu veḷiyēṟit taṉ talivaṉōṭu porunti vāḻavirukkum kuṟippit talivi tōḻikku uṇartti aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷ eṉka.

ippāṭal kuṟiñci eṉ tiṇiyum tōḻikkut talivi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉ tuṟiyum eṉka.





66 īka maṉṟō īkam

﻿ māṉamum uyirum vāḻvum karuti

vāṉamum nilamum nīrum kaṭantu

kāṉamum pukuntu kaḷattiṉum nerintu

tāṉum kuṭumpum iṉamum iṭarppaṭa



āyiram āyiram iḷiyarum peṇṭirum





5





māyvatum tirivatum ākiya nilikoḷ

īḻat tamiḻar iṭiyiṉil tōṉṟik

kāḻtta neñciṉ kaṉṉi iḷamuki -

taṉṉiyum iḻantu tamariyum iḻanta



aṉṉik kulattōr aṟaṅkūr maṟatti





10





‘tāṉu'veṉ peyariṉaḷ taṉṉiṉam aḻitta

vīṇaṉuk ketirā vekuṇṭa veñciṉam

neñciṉum uyiriṉum nilitta niṉivoṭu

orutaṉi niṉṟē ūrnaṭu cititta

īka maṉṟō īkam

ākumō ulakavaḷ aḻivilāp pukaḻkkē!





poḻippu :

taṉṉuṭiya māṉattiyum uyiriyum vāḻkkiyiyum kāttuk koḷḷak karuti, vāṉattiyum nilattiyum nīriyum kaṭantu (veḷināṭu ēkiyum, (avvāṟu ēkaviyalātavarkaḷ aṅkēyē uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil pukuntu (oḷintu)m pōrāṭṭak kaḷattil (cikkic) citintum uyiriḻantum, avaravarum, avaravar kuṭumpa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ iṉamakkaḷum iṭarkaḷ paṭukiṉṟa, pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa iḷiñarkaḷum peṇṭirkaḷum, māṇṭu pōvatum, (ūr ūrāka, nāṭu nāṭāka ētilikaḷākat) tirivatum ākiya nilikaḷik koṇṭuḷḷa īḻattil uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ naṭuvē piṟantu tōṉṟi, vayiram pōṉṟa (uṟutiyāṉa) neñcuṭiya, kaṉṉip paruvattiṉ iḷami moṭṭup pōṉṟavaḷum, taṉṉiyum (kaṟpaḷavil) iḻantum,taṉ kuṭumpattiṉariyum uṟaviṉariyum cāvaḷavil iḻantum viṭṭa - tāymik kulattil tōṉṟiya aṟavuṇarvu kūrtaluṟṟu maṟavuṇarvāy niṉṟa maṟattiyum-ākiya tāṉu'eṉṉum peyari uṭiyavaḷ. taṉ iṉamākiya tamiḻiṉatti aḻittu varum vīṇāṉa āṭciyic ceytavaṉākiya irācīvukku etirāka, vekuṇṭeḻunta (veppam mikunta paḻivāṅkattakka) kaṭuñciṉam taṉ neñcattilum, uyirilum (kalantu) nilittuviṭṭa māṟāta eṇṇattoṭu, tāṉ oruttiyē taṉittu niṉṟāḷāki, ūrmakkaḷukku naṭuvil avaṉic citit taḻittut taṉṉiyum iḻantu niṉṟa īkamaṉṟō (maṟṟa ellā niliyilum piṟar ceyta īkaṅkaḷi viṭac ciṟanta) īkamākum! ivvakiyil avaḷ peṟṟa aḻivilāta pukaḻkku ivvulakamē īṭu ākumō? (ākātu eṉka.)

virippu:

ippāṭal puṟattuṟiyic cārntatu. tamiḻīḻattil nikaḻntu varukiṉṟa viṭutalip pōrāṭṭak kālattu iṭiyil nikaḻnta, intiyāviṉ talimiyamiccarākavirunta irācīv eṉṟa intirāviṉ makaṉum, cavakarlāl nēruviṉ peyaraṉum, ilaṅkit tamiḻarkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi, irācīv ilaṅkikku aṉuppiya amitippaṭi eṉṟa tamiḻiṉa aḻivup paṭiyāl tākkuṇṭa tāṉu eṉṟa or iḷam peṇ taṉ uṭaliṟ piṇitta veṭikuṇṭuṭaṉ neruṅki mōti aḻikkavum aḻiyavum paṭṭa aḷaviṭaṟkariya īkac cāvip pōṟṟip pukaḻntu kūṟiyatākum ippāṭal.

tāṉu eṉṉum peyariṉaḷ, taṉṉiṉam aḻitta vīṇaṉākiya irācīvukku etirāka vekuṇṭu eḻuntu, ilaṅkiyiṉiṉṟu tamiḻakam pōntu, taṉi oruttiyāka, ūrmakkaḷ naṭuvil niṉṟu veṭikuṇṭu piṇitta uṭampuṭaṉ avaṉi mōtiyaḻittalāl, tāṉum taṉ iṉṉiḷavuyir nīttuk koṇṭa īkamaṟam, vēṟenta īkattiṉum taṉicciṟappum perumiyum koṇṭatu ākaliṉ atu aḻivilāp pukaḻ peṟṟatu eṉka.

ippāṭṭu nāṉku vēṟāṉa ceyaṟpaṭi nilikaḷik koṇṭatu. āṉālum nāṉku ceyalkaḷum paṭippaṭiyē oṉṟuṭaṉ oṉṟu toṭarpu koṇṭatu.

mutalatu, tamiḻīḻattil iṉattākkam nērntu, ciṅkaḷa iṉattālum, irācīv intiyāviṉiṉṟu amitippaṭi eṉṉum peyarāl aṉuppi vitta aḻimpup paṭiyālum tākkamuṟṟa tamiḻīḻat tamiḻar, atu poṟātu, taṅkaḷiṉ māṉattiyum uyiriyum vāḻviyum kāttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭi, vāṉūrti vaḻi vāṉiyum, iyaṅkikaḷ vaḻi nilattiyum, kappal vaḻikkaṭaliyum kaṭantu veḷināṭukaḷukku ēkiyatum,

iraṇṭatu, avvāṟu veḷināṭu ēkuvataṟkiyalāta tamiḻa makkaḷ, aṅkuḷḷa kāṉakaṅkaḷil pukuntu oḷintu vāḻtalum, iḷavōrāka uḷḷa pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum viṭutalip pōrāḷikaḷuṭaṉ pōrāṭṭak kaḷattil virumpiyō virumpāmalō, pōril īṭupaṭṭu, uṭalkaḷ cititalum uyirkaḷ cititalum āki iṭar paṭutalum,

mūṉṟatu, avvāṟu iṭarppāṭuṟṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷ oṉṟiṉic cērnta tāṉu’ eṉṉum oru kaḻi iḷampeṇ, aṅkuḷḷa paṭiyiṉarāl kaṟpiḻantum, avar kuṭumpattiṉar aḻintu paṭṭatum,

nāṉkatu, avviṭarppaṭṭa tāṉu, aṟavuṇarvu taḻuviya maṟavuṇarvu koṇṭu, tāṉum taṉ kuṭumpum iṉamum tākkuṇṭa koṭumi poṟātavaḷāki, vekuṇṭu avaṟṟukkellām kāraṇaṉākiya irācīv eṉṉum vīṇaṉi aḻittoḻikkum uḷḷattiṉum uyiriṉum nilitta paḻivāṅkum kaṭuñciṉattuṭaṉ intiyā vantu, tamiḻakattil cuṟṟuc celavu mēṟkoṇṭirunta irācīvi, taṉ uṭalil veṭikuṇṭu potintirukka neruṅki mōti, makkaḷellām kūṭiyirunta kūṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil, aḻittum aḻivuṇṭum māyntatum -

- ākiya nāṉku ceyalkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkik kūṟiyatākum ippāṭal.

in nāṉku ceyalkaḷum oṉṟiṉ oṉṟu avalam mikuntatu. iṟutic ceyalākiya tāṉuviṉ taṉṉaḻivu maṟam, koṭiyaṉ oruvaṉi aḻittatōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭatākaliṉ īkavuṇarvāl poruntiya pukaḻ koṇṭatu. ataṉāl ulaki īṭu koḷḷāp perumi cāṉṟatu.

itil, tāṉu veṉṉum vīrat tamiḻppeṇ, kāḻtta neñciṉ kaṉṉi iḷamuki eṉṟum, aṉṉikkuk kulattu or aṟaṅkūr maṟatti eṉṟum, pukaḻappeṟuvatu, avaḷiṉ iḷam paruvattiyum, mutu ceyaliyum iṇittup pārttup perumi cāṟṟuvatu.

oru taṉi niṉṟē ūrnaṭu cititta īkam'eṉṉum toṭaruḷ varum, oru taṉi eṉpatu, avaḷ taṉittu niṉṟa cemmāppiyum, ūr naṭu cititta’ eṉpatu, yārukkum teriyāmal maṟimukamāka aṉṟi ellārkkum muṉṉaḻitta centuṇiviyum, īkam’ eṉpatu, avaḷ ulaka veṟukkiyiyum kūṟi, avaḷ maṉavuṇarviyum, iṉavuṇarviyum, koḷki niliyiyum, iḷamip paruvattiyum oruṅkē pulappaṭutti vīrattiyum avalattiyum iṇittuk kūṟiyatu eṉka!

māṉamum uyirum vāḻvum karuti - tamiḻīḻa viṭutalip pōrāṭṭam toṭaṅkiya kālattiliruntē, aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻp potu makkaḷukkup palavakiyāṉa tākkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: ēṟpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.

at tākkaṅkaḷāl toṭarntu uyiraḻivukaḷ nērntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki nalaṉkaḷum cīraḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṭiyiruppukaḷ tī vittuk koḷuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉ. cottukaḷ koḷḷi yaṭikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ pāliyal vaṉmuṟikku āḷākiṉṟaṉar. itil ciṟumiyar, iḷam peṇkaḷ, tirumaṇam āṉa maṅkiyar, karuvuṟṟa tāymārkaḷ, mutuiḷam peṇṭirkaḷ eṉṉum vēṟupāṭukaḷ illi : pāliyal tākkaṅkaḷukkup piṉṉar avarkaḷ koli ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟit taṭukka muyaṟci ceyyum āṭavarkaḷ veṭṭik kollappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iḷiñarkaḷ aṉivarumē pōrāḷikaḷ allatu pōrāḷikaḷāka māṟakkūṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṉum karuttil paṭittuṟit taṅkil(mukām)kaḷukku aḻittuc cellappaṭṭuk kollappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.

ivvāṟu aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku māṉa aḻivum uyiraḻivum vāḻvuc citivum ēṟpaṭuvatāl, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu taṅkaḷik kāttuk koḷḷak karuti, avarkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷukkut tappic cella vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāka ākukiṉṟaṉar.

vāṉamum nilamum nīrum kaṭantu - eṉavē, tamiḻarkaḷ vāṉūrti vaḻiyāka vāṉ elliyik kaṭantum, iyaṅkikaḷ pēruntukaḷ vaḻiyākavum, naṭantum tākkam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟa nilap pakutiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟṟiṭaṅkaḷukkup peyarntum, kappal vaḻiyākak kaṭal elliyik kaṭantum ayal nāṭukaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.

kāṉamum pukuntu kaḷattiṉum nerintu-avvāṟu cella iyalātavarkaḷ, nakaraṅkaḷil nērum tākkaṅkaḷukku añcit taṅkaḷik kāttuk koḷḷak kāṭukaḷil oṭi oḷintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷil iḷiñarkaḷākavum āṭavarkaḷākavum uḷḷavarkaḷ pōrāḷikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu, pōrāṭṭak kaḷaṅkaḷilum ceṉṟu citipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. nerintu - nerukkuṇṭu - citipaṭṭu uṟuppukaḷ iḻantu uyiriḻantu.

tāṉum kuṭumpum iṉamum iṭarppaṭa- ivvakiyil aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷil ovvoruvarum, avar avar kuṭumpaṅkaḷum iṉamum palavakiyilum iṭarppāṭukaḷ eyta

āyiram - īḻattamiḻar - pala āyirakkaṇakkil iḷiñarkaḷum peṇṭirum, ivvāṟu ulakam muḻuvatum pōy ētiliyarkaḷāka nāṭu nāṭākat tirintu varuvatum, pala nilikaḷil māyntu pōvatum ākiya nilikaḷil uḷḷa īḻat tamiḻarkaḷ.

iṭiyiṉil tōṉṟi - avarkaḷil tāṉum oruttiyākat tōṉṟi.

kāḻtta neñciṉ kaṉṉi iḷamuki - vayiram pōl uṟutiyāṉa neñcuṭiya, kaṉṉip paruvattiṉ malara vēṇṭiya, iḷami cāṉṟa moṭṭup pōṉṟavaḷ.

taṉṉiyum iḻantu - irācīv aṉuppiya paṭittuṟiyiṉarāl taṉ perumiyi iḻantu - cīrkulivuvaṟṟu.

tamariyum iḻanta- taṉ peṟṟōriyum uṟṟāriyum akkāval paṭiyiṉarāl iḻanta, tamartammavar peṟṟōrum uṟṟārum. tāṉuviṉ kuṭumpattavarkaḷ intiya amitip paṭiyiṉarāl aḻik kappaṭṭaṉar. tāṉuvum avarkaḷāl koṭumiyākak kaṟpaḻikkappaṭṭavaḷ. intak koṭuñ ceyalkaḷ tām avaḷip paḻivāṅkum uṇarvukkut taḷḷiṉa. eṉiṉ atu mikiyākātu.

amitip paṭiyiṉar ivvāṟu taṉakkum taṉ kuṭumpattiṉarkkum maṭṭumallāmal pala nūṟu kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkum tīṅku ceytatu, tāṉuvāl poṟuttukkoḷḷa muṭiyāta valluṇarvi avaḷukkut tantirukka vēṇṭum.

taṉ uṟṟār uṟaviṉar uyir aḻiviṟkum, taṉṉuṭiya māṉa aḻiviṟkum, amitip paṭiyiṉar kāraṇamāṉavarkaḷāka iruntālum, avarkaḷip paḻivāṅkuvatu taṉakku iyalātu eṉak kaṇṭāḷ, tāṉu. eṉavē, avarkaḷit tamiḻīḻattiṟku aṉuppi vittu, inniliyi uruvākkiya intiyat talimiyamiccar irācīvē muḻuvatum paḻivāṅkattakkavar eṉa, tāṉu uṟuti koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.
